</style>

</head>

<body>

<div class="header-container">
    <div class="header-info">
        <div class="header-title">TEST MANAGER REPORT</div>
        <div class="header-project">Project: {project}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="header-logo">{logo_html}</div>
</div>

<table class="summary-table">
<tr><th colspan="2">Traceability</th></tr>
<tr><td>SFC</td><td>{sfc}</td></tr>
<tr><td>Operation</td><td>{operation}</td></tr>
<tr><td>Date</td><td>{datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}</td></tr>
</table>

<br>

<table class="summary-table">
<tr><th>Total</th><th>PASS</th><th>FAIL</th><th>SKIPPED</th></tr>
<tr><td>{total}</td><td>{passed}</td><td>{failed}</td><td>{skipped}</td></tr>
</table>
"""

        for name, result in results.items():
            status = getattr(result, "status", "UNKNOWN")
            verification = result.verification if hasattr(result, "verification") else {}
            parsed_data = result.parsed_data if hasattr(result, "parsed_data") else {}

            display_status = status
            css = "pass"

            if (status == "UNKNOWN"
                    and "No hay límites definidos" in verification.get("message", "")):
                display_status = "SKIPPED"
                css = "skipped"
            elif status == "FAIL":
                css = "fail"
            elif status == "PASS":
                css = "pass"

            html += f"""

<div class="test-block">

<div class="test-title">
{name}
</div>

<div class="test-summary">

<b>Status:</b> <span class="{css}">{display_status}</span>

&nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp;

<b>Board:</b> {getattr(result, "board_type", "")}

&nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp;

<b>Duration:</b> {getattr(result, "duration_ms", 0):.0f} ms

</div>
"""

            details = verification.get("details", {}) if verification else {}
            show_message = True

            if (
                ("temperature" in details and "humidity" in details)
                or ("voltage" in details and "current" in details)
                or ("expected" in details and "actual" in details and len(details) == 2)
                or ("value" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                or ("temp" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                or ("score" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                or (name == "LPC")
                or (name in ["PCIe Iperf Test", "USB3_3 Iperf Test",
                             "USB3_2 Iperf Test", "USB2 Iperf Test"])
                or (name in ["PCIe number of port", "USB3_3 number of port",
                             "USB2 number of port", "USB3_2 number of port"])
                or (name == "AB Erase DID")
                or (name in ["USB3 to USB2", "USB2 to USB3"])
                or (isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                    and "bus_width" in parsed_data.get("data", {})
                    and "clock" in parsed_data.get("data", {}))
            ):
                show_message = False

            is_iperf_test = name in [
                "PCIe Iperf Test", "USB3_3 Iperf Test",
                "USB3_2 Iperf Test", "USB2 Iperf Test"
            ]

            is_configuration_test = name in ["USB3 to USB2", "USB2 to USB3"]

            is_number_of_port_test = name in [
                "PCIe number of port", "USB3_3 number of port",
                "USB2 number of port", "USB3_2 number of port"
            ]

            if verification:
                has_ports_renderer = ("ports" in details)

                # CAMBIO: mostrar verificación para iperf/CSI/config FAIL
                should_show = (
                    not is_number_of_port_test
                    and (
                        (
                            not is_iperf_test
                            and not has_ports_renderer
                            and (show_message or len(details) > 0)
                        )
                        or (is_iperf_test and status == "FAIL")
                        or (is_configuration_test and status == "FAIL")
                    )
                )

                if should_show:
                    html += """
<div class="section-title">
Verification
</div>

<table class="detail-table">
"""

                    # CAMBIO: mostrar message también en iperf FAIL
                    if show_message or (is_iperf_test and status == "FAIL") or (is_configuration_test and status == "FAIL"):
                        html += f"""
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">
Message
</td>
<td>
{verification.get("message", "")}
</td>
</tr>
"""

                    html += """
</table>
"""

                details = verification.get("details", {})

                # PCB ID
                if ("expected" in details and "actual" in details and len(details) == 2):
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_expected_actual_table(
                        "PCB Verification",
                        details.get("expected", ""),
                        details.get("actual", ""),
                        display_status,
                        css
                    )

                # Generic Value
                elif ("value" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details):
                    rows = [{
                        "name": details.get("sapme_key", "Value").replace("CHECK ", "").title(),
                        "low": details.get("low", ""),
                        "actual": details.get("value", ""),
                        "high": details.get("high", ""),
                        "result": display_status,
                        "css": css
                    }]
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_measurement_table(
                        "Measurement Verification", rows
                    )

                # DDR
                elif ("score" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details):
                    rows = [{
                        "name": details.get("sapme_key", "DDR VALUE"),
                        "low": details.get("low", ""),
                        "actual": details.get("score", ""),
                        "high": details.get("high", ""),
                        "result": display_status,
                        "css": css
                    }]
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_measurement_table(
                        "DDR Verification", rows
                    )

                # SMBus
                elif ("temp" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details):
                    rows = [{
                        "name": "Temperature",
                        "low": details.get("low", ""),
                        "actual": details.get("temp", ""),
                        "high": details.get("high", ""),
                        "result": display_status,
                        "css": css
                    }]
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_measurement_table(
                        "SMBus Verification", rows
                    )

                # CPU INFO
                elif (isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                      and "data" in parsed_data
                      and isinstance(parsed_data["data"], dict)
                      and "vendor_id" in parsed_data["data"]):
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_cpu_info_table(parsed_data["data"])

                # MMC
                elif (isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                      and "data" in parsed_data
                      and isinstance(parsed_data["data"], dict)
                      and "bus_width" in parsed_data["data"]
                      and "clock" in parsed_data["data"]):
                    mmc_title = "MMC Information"
                    if "SDIO" in name.upper():
                        mmc_title = "SDIO Information"
                    elif "EMMC" in name.upper():
                        mmc_title = "EMMC Information"
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_mmc_info_table(
                        mmc_title, parsed_data["data"]
                    )

                # Thermal
                if ("temperature" in details and "humidity" in details):
                    rows = [
                        {"name": "Temperature", "low": "", "actual": f"{details['temperature']} °C",
                         "high": "", "result": display_status, "css": css},
                        {"name": "Humidity", "low": "", "actual": f"{details['humidity']} %",
                         "high": "", "result": display_status, "css": css}
                    ]
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_measurement_table(
                        "Thermal Measurements", rows
                    )

                # Current / Voltage
                elif ("voltage" in details and "current" in details):
                    rows = [
                        {"name": "Voltage", "low": details.get("voltage_low", ""),
                         "actual": details.get("voltage", ""),
                         "high": details.get("voltage_high", ""),
                         "result": display_status, "css": css},
                        {"name": "Current", "low": details.get("current_low", ""),
                         "actual": details.get("current", ""),
                         "high": details.get("current_high", ""),
                         "result": display_status, "css": css}
                    ]
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_measurement_table(
                        "Current / Voltage Measurements", rows
                    )

                # Ports
                ports = details.get("ports", [])
                if ports:
                    html += """
<div class="section-title">
Measured Data
</div>
"""
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_ports(ports)

                # Expected / Actual genéricos
                show_expected_actual = True
                if ("expected" in details and "actual" in details and len(details) == 2):
                    show_expected_actual = False

                if show_expected_actual and "expected" in details:
                    html += f"""
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">Expected</td>
<td>{details.get("expected")}</td>
</tr>
"""

                if show_expected_actual and "actual" in details:
                    html += f"""
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">Actual</td>
<td>{details.get("actual")}</td>
</tr>
"""

                # Limits
                show_limits = True
                if (("value" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or ("score" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or ("temp" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)):
                    show_limits = False

                low = details.get("low")
                high = details.get("high")

                if show_limits and (low is not None or high is not None):
                    html += f"""
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">Low Limit</td>
<td>{low}</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">High Limit</td>
<td>{high}</td>
</tr>
"""

                # Additional details
                remaining_details = {}
                for key, value in details.items():
                    if key in ("expected", "actual", "low", "high", "ports",
                               "channels", "adc"):
                        continue
                    remaining_details[key] = value

                if (("temperature" in details and "humidity" in details)
                        or ("voltage" in details and "current" in details)
                        or ("value" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or ("score" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or ("temp" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or ("sapme_key" in details and "actual" in details and name == "AB Erase DID")):
                    remaining_details = {}

                if remaining_details:
                    html += """
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">Additional Details</td>
<td>
"""
                    for key, value in remaining_details.items():
                        html += f"<b>{key}</b>: {value}<br>"
                    html += """
</td>
</tr>
"""

            # Test Results / Data
            show_test_results = True

            if isinstance(parsed_data, dict):
                details = verification.get("details", {})

                if (("temperature" in details and "humidity" in details)
                        or ("voltage" in details and "current" in details)
                        or ("expected" in details and "actual" in details and len(details) == 2)
                        or ("vendor_id" in parsed_data.get("data", {}))
                        or ("value" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or ("score" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or ("temp" in details and "low" in details and "high" in details)
                        or (parsed_data.get("data", {}).get("message") == "COMMAND ACCEPT")
                        or (name == "LPC")
                        or (isinstance(details.get("ports", []), list)
                            and len(details.get("ports", [])) > 0)
                        or (name in ["USB3 to USB2", "USB2 to USB3"])
                        or (name == "AB Erase Did")
                        or (isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                            and "bus_width" in parsed_data.get("data", {})
                            and "clock" in parsed_data.get("data", {}))):
                    show_test_results = False

            if parsed_data and show_test_results:
                html += """
<div class="section-title">
TestResults / Data
</div>
"""

                # SPI
                if (isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                        and "steps" in parsed_data
                        and len(parsed_data["steps"]) > 0
                        and "device_id" in parsed_data["steps"][0]
                        .get("verification", {}).get("details", {})):
                    spi_limits = app_state.sapme_limits.get("SPI", {})
                    rows = []
                    for step in parsed_data["steps"]:
                        details_spi = step.get("verification", {}).get("details", {})
                        cmd = step.get("cmd", "")
                        device = cmd.replace("websocket_test.sh spi ", "").strip()
                        sapme_key = f"SPI_{device}_ID"
                        expected = spi_limits.get(sapme_key, {}).get("high", "")
                        actual = details_spi.get("device_id", "")
                        rows.append({
                            "device": f"SPI {device}",
                            "expected": expected,
                            "actual": actual,
                            "result": step.get("verification", {}).get("status", "")
                        })
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_spi_table(rows)

                # I2S
                elif (name == "I2S"
                      and isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                      and "steps" in parsed_data):
                    i2s_limits = app_state.sapme_limits.get("I2S", {})
                    rows = []
                    for step in parsed_data["steps"]:
                        details_i2s = step.get("verification", {}).get("details", {})
                        if "score" not in details_i2s:
                            continue
                        cmd = step.get("cmd", "")
                        match = re.search(r"i2s\s+(\d+)\s+1\s+1", cmd)
                        if not match:
                            continue
                        channel_num = match.group(1)
                        sapme_key = f"CHECKI2S_{channel_num}_VALUE"
                        limits_i2s = i2s_limits.get(sapme_key, {})
                        rows.append({
                            "name": f"I2S {channel_num}",
                            "low": limits_i2s.get("low", ""),
                            "actual": details_i2s.get("score", ""),
                            "high": limits_i2s.get("high", ""),
                            "result": step.get("verification", {}).get("status", "")
                        })
                    if rows:
                        html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_i2s_table(rows)

                # I2C
                elif (isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                      and "steps" in parsed_data
                      and len(parsed_data["steps"]) > 0
                      and "temp" in parsed_data["steps"][0]
                      .get("verification", {}).get("details", {})):
                    rows = []
                    for step in parsed_data["steps"]:
                        details_i2c = step.get("verification", {}).get("details", {})
                        sensor_name = details_i2c.get("sapme_key", "").replace(
                            "12C_TEMPERATURE_", "Sensor "
                        )
                        rows.append({
                            "sensor": sensor_name,
                            "low": details_i2c.get("low", ""),
                            "actual": details_i2c.get("temp", ""),
                            "high": details_i2c.get("high", ""),
                            "result": step.get("verification", {}).get("status", "")
                        })
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_i2c_table(rows)

                # GPIO
                elif (name == "GPIO"
                      and isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                      and "steps" in parsed_data):
                    patterns = {}
                    for step in parsed_data["steps"]:
                        details_gpio = step.get("verification", {}).get("details", {})
                        if "patterns" in details_gpio:
                            patterns.update(details_gpio["patterns"])
                        elif "pattern" in details_gpio and "actual" in details_gpio:
                            patterns[details_gpio["pattern"]] = {
                                "high": details_gpio.get("expected", ""),
                                "actual": details_gpio.get("actual", ""),
                                "status": step.get("verification", {}).get("status", "")
                            }
                    rows = []
                    for pattern_name, pattern_data in patterns.items():
                        if not (pattern_name.endswith("_0-15")
                                or pattern_name.endswith("_16-31")
                                or pattern_name.endswith("_32-41")):
                            continue
                        rows.append({
                            "pattern": pattern_name,
                            "expected": pattern_data.get("high", ""),
                            "actual": pattern_data.get("actual", ""),
                            "result": pattern_data.get("status", "")
                        })
                    if rows:
                        html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_gpio_table(rows)

                # PWM
                elif (name == "PWM"
                      and isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                      and "steps" in parsed_data):
                    rows = []
                    for step in parsed_data["steps"]:
                        cmd = step.get("cmd", "")
                        command_name = cmd.replace("websocket_test.sh ", "")
                        result_step = step.get("verification", {}).get("status", "")
                        rows.append({
                            "command": command_name,
                            "result": result_step
                        })
                    html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_pwm_table(rows)

                # ADC
                elif (isinstance(parsed_data, dict) and "steps" in parsed_data):
                    for idx, step in enumerate(parsed_data["steps"]):
                        html += f"""
<table class="detail-table">
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">ADC Step</td>
<td>{idx + 1}</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">Command</td>
<td>{step.get("cmd", "")}</td>
</tr>
</table>

<br>
"""
                        verification_adc = step.get("verification", {})
                        html += f"""
<div class="adc-summary">
<b>Step:</b> {idx + 1}
&nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp;
<b>Status:</b> {verification_adc.get("status", "")}
&nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp;
{verification_adc.get("message", "")}
</div>

<br>
"""
                        details_adc = verification_adc.get("details", {})
                        adc_name = details_adc.get("adc", "")
                        channels = details_adc.get("channels", [])
                        if channels:
                            rows = []
                            for channel in channels:
                                rows.append({
                                    "name": channel.get("channel", ""),
                                    "low": channel.get("low", ""),
                                    "high": channel.get("high", ""),
                                    "actual": channel.get("actual", ""),
                                    "result": channel.get("result", "")
                                })
                            html += HtmlReportBuilder.render_subsequence_table(
                                adc_name, rows
                            )

                # Generic data
                elif (isinstance(parsed_data, dict)
                      and "data" in parsed_data
                      and isinstance(parsed_data["data"], dict)):
                    data_rows = parsed_data["data"]
                    if len(data_rows) <= 20:
                        html += """
<table class="detail-table">
"""
                        for key, value in data_rows.items():
                            html += f"""
<tr>
<td class="detail-label">{key}</td>
<td>{value}</td>
</tr>
"""
                        html += """
</table>
"""

            html += """
</div>
"""

        html += """
</body>
</html>
"""

        return html

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Repositorio para buttons.json"""

import json
from pathlib import Path
from core.utils.resource_path import resource_path

class ButtonRepository:
    """Carga y consulta configuración de botones"""
    
    def __init__(self, config_path: str = "buttons.json"):
        self.config_path = resource_path(config_path)
        self._buttons = None
        self._buttons_by_label = {}
        self._load()
    
    def _load(self):
        """Carga el archivo JSON"""
        if not self.config_path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"No se encuentra: {self.config_path}")
        
        with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            self._buttons = json.load(f)
        
        # Indexar por label para búsqueda rápida
        self._buttons_by_label = {
            b['label']: b for b in self._buttons
        }
    
    def get_all(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Obtiene todos los botones"""
        return self._buttons
    
    def get_by_label(self, label: str) -> dict | None:
        """Obtiene un botón por su label"""
        return self._buttons_by_label.get(label)
    
    def get_test_names(self) -> list[str]:
        """Obtiene todos los nombres de pruebas"""
        return list(self._buttons_by_label.keys())
    
    def get_sequence_buttons(self, sequence_name: str) -> list[dict]:
        """Obtiene los botones de una secuencia"""
        button = self.get_by_label(sequence_name)
        if not button:
            return []
        
        action = button.get('action', {})
        if action.get('type') != 'sequence_buttons':
            return []
        
        button_names = action.get('buttons', [])
        return [self.get_by_label(name) for name in button_names if self.get_by_label(name)]
    
    def reload(self):
        """Recarga el archivo"""
        self._buttons = None
        self._buttons_by_label = {}
        self._load()

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\repositories\limits_repository.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Repositorio para limits.json"""

import json
import logging
from typing import Any
from core.state.app_state import app_state
from core.utils.resource_path import resource_path
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
DEBUG = False
def debug_print(*args, **kwargs):

    if DEBUG:

        print(*args, **kwargs)
class LimitsRepository:
    """Carga y consulta límites de pruebas"""
    
    def __init__(self, config_path: str = "limits.json"):
        debug_print(resource_path)
        self.config_path = resource_path(
    config_path
)
        self._limits = {}
        self._load()
    
    def _load(self):
        """Carga el archivo JSON"""
        if not self.config_path.exists():
            logger.warning(f"No se encuentra: {self.config_path}, usando límites por defecto")
            self._limits = self._get_default_limits()
            return
        
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                self._limits = json.load(f)
            logger.info(f"Límites cargados desde {self.config_path}")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Error cargando límites: {e}")
            self._limits = self._get_default_limits()
    
    def _get_default_limits(self) -> dict:
        """Retorna límites por defecto si no hay archivo"""
        return {
            "Check PCB ID": {"type": "exists"},

    def get_limits(
        self,
        test_name: str
    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        debug_print(
            f"[LIMITS] Solicitando límites para: {test_name}"
        )
        #
        # SAPME primero
        #
        debug_print(
            f"SAPME_READY = {app_state.sapme_ready}"
        )
        if (
            app_state.sapme_ready
            and
            app_state.sapme_limits
        ):

            sapme_limits = self._get_sapme_limits(
                test_name
            )

            if sapme_limits:
                return sapme_limits

        #
        # Fallback limits.json
        #

        return self._limits.get(
            test_name
        )
    
    def get_all(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Obtiene todos los límites"""
        return self._limits
    
    def has_limits(self, test_name: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica si existen límites para una prueba"""
        return test_name in self._limits
    
    def reload(self):
        """Recarga el archivo"""
        self._limits = {}
        self._load()

    def get_limits(
        self,
        test_name: str
    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:

        #
        # SAPME tiene prioridad
        #
        debug_print(
            f"[SAPME] Solicitando límites para: {test_name}"
        )
        debug_print(
            f"SAPME_READY = {app_state.sapme_ready}"
        )
        if (
            app_state.sapme_ready
            and
            app_state.sapme_limits
        ):

            sapme = app_state.sapme_limits

            #
            # ADC
            #
            if test_name == "ADC":

                adc = sapme.get(
                    "ADC",
                    {}
                )

                if adc:

                    debug_print(
                        "[SAPME] ADC solicitado"
                    )

                    return adc
            #
            # Kernel Version
            #

            debug_print(
                f"[SAPME] Solicitando límites para: {test_name}"
            )

            if test_name == "Kernel Version":

                cpu = sapme.get(
                    "CPU",
                    {}
                )

                data = cpu.get(
                    "CHECK KERNEL VERSION"
                )

                if data:

                    return {
                        "expected":
                            str(
                                data["high"]
                            ).replace(
                                ",",
                                "."
                            )
                    }

            #
            # CBHW Version
            #

            if test_name == "CBHW Version":

                debug_print(
                    "[SAPME] CBHW Version solicitado"
                )

                version = sapme.get(
                    "VERSION",
                    {}
                )

                data = version.get(
                    "CHECK CB HW VERSION"
                )

                if data:

                    debug_print(
                        f"[SAPME] Valor encontrado: {data['high']}"
                    )

                    return {
                        "expected":
                            str(
                                data["high"]
                            )
                            .replace(
                                "CB VER:",
                                ""
                            )
                            .strip()
                            .upper()
                    }

            #
            # CBSW Version
            #

            if test_name == "CBSW Version":

                debug_print(
                    "[SAPME] CBSW Version solicitado"
                )

                version = sapme.get(
                    "VERSION",
                    {}
                )

                data = version.get(
                    "CHECK CB SW VERSION"
                )

                if data:

                    debug_print(
                        f"[SAPME] Valor encontrado: {data['high']}"
                    )

                    return {
                        "expected":
                            str(
                                data["high"]
                            )
                            .replace(
                                ",",
                                "."
                            )
                            .strip()
                            .upper()
                    }

            #
            # ABHW Version
            #

            if test_name == "ABHW Version":

                debug_print(
                    "[SAPME] ABHW Version solicitado"
                )

                version = sapme.get(
                    "VERSION",
                    {}
                )

                data = version.get(
                    "CHECK AB HW VERSION"
                )

                if data:

                    debug_print(
                        f"[SAPME] Valor encontrado: {data['high']}"
                    )

                    return {
                        "expected":
                            str(
                                data["high"]
                            )
                            .replace(
                                "AB VER:",
                                ""
                            )
                            .strip()
                            .upper()
                    }

            #
            # ABSW Version
            #

            if test_name == "ABSW Version":

                debug_print(
                    "[SAPME] ABSW Version solicitado"
                )

                version = sapme.get(
                    "VERSION",
                    {}
                )

                data = version.get(
                    "CHECK AB SW VERSION"
                )

                if data:

                    debug_print(
                        f"[SAPME] Valor encontrado: {data['high']}"

                    return {
                        "expected":

                                data["high"]

                                ",",

                    }

            #
            # cursens_12vtoj6
            #
            if test_name == "cursens_12vtoj6":

                power = sapme.get(
                    "POWER",
                    {}
                )

                voltage = power.get(

                        ),
                        "voltage_max": float(
                            voltage["high"]
                        ),
                        "current_min": float(
                            current["low"]
                        ),
                        "current_max": float(
                            current["high"]
                        )
                    }

            #
            # cursens_hsbridge
            #
            if test_name == "cursens_hsbridge":

                    "POWER",
                    {}
                )

                        ),
                        "voltage_max": float(
                            voltage["high"]
                        ),

                            current["low"]

                        "current_max": float(
                            current["high"]
                        )
                    }
            #
            # Thermal Port 0

                    "THERMAL",

                    "CB_BOARD_TEMP"

                        ),
                        "temperature_max": float(
                            temp["high"]
                        ),
                        "humidity_min": float(
                            hum["low"]
                        ),
                        "humidity_max": float(
                            hum["high"]
                        )
                    }

            #

            #

            #

            #
            if test_name in [
                "USB3_3 number of port",

                    "MISC",

                    "USB3.0_2.0 CHECK THE NUMBER OF"

                        ).strip().upper()

            #
            # Configuration USB
            #
            if test_name in [
                "USB3 to USB2",
                "USB2 to USB3"
            ]:
                return {
                    "expected_result": "CONFIGURATION"
                }

            #
            # Fallback LOCAL
            #
        return self._limits.get(
            test_name
        )


================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\repositories\project_repository.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3

import json

from core.utils.resource_path import resource_path


class ProjectRepository:

    def __init__(
        self,
        config_path: str = "projects.json"
    ):

        self.config_path = resource_path(
            config_path
        )

        self._projects = {}

        self._load()

    def _load(self):

        if not self.config_path.exists():

            raise FileNotFoundError(
                f"No se encuentra: {self.config_path}"
            )

        with open(
            self.config_path,
            "r",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as file:

            self._projects = json.load(
                file
            )

    def get_project(
        self,
        part_number: str
    ) -> str:

        return self._projects.get(
            part_number,
            "UNKNOWN"
        )

    def reload(self):

        self._load()

    def get_project_from_sfc(
        self,
        sfc: str
    ) -> str:

        for suffix, project in self._projects.items():

            clean_suffix = suffix.lstrip("0")

            if sfc.endswith(clean_suffix):

                return project

        return "UNKNOWN"

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\services\diagnostic_engine.py
================================================================================

import re
 
class DiagnosticEngine:
    """
    Motor de reglas basado en el reporte oficial de Harman 
    (LGA Check AB Connection - Failure Analysis Report)
    """
 
    @staticmethod
    def analyze_ab_log_only(log_text: str) -> dict:
        """
        FILTRO 1: Analiza SOLO el log. Si no encuentra nada, devuelve UNKNOWN.
        """
        log_upper = log_text.upper()
 
        # 1. Modo 03: Falla en SDRAM (Error de MRC)
        if "MRC" in log_upper and ("ERROR" in log_upper or "WARN" in log_upper):
            return {
                "mode": "03",
                "status": "FAIL",
                "diagnosis": "Falla en el sistema SDRAM. El procesador no puede comunicarse con las memorias.",
                "action": "Revisar corto en el procesador Intel (IC10500) o reemplazar memorias SDRAM (IC10651)."
            }
 
        # 2. Modo 04: Daño en el Procesador Intel (Kernel Panic por log)
        if "ACPI MEMORY OR I/O RESET_REG" in log_upper or "MACHINE CHECK EXCEPTION" in log_upper:
            return {
                "mode": "04",
                "status": "FAIL",
                "diagnosis": "Daño interno en el procesador Intel. Error de hardware o acceso a memoria Flash.",
                "action": "Reemplazar el procesador Intel (IC10500)."
            }
 
        # 3. Falla de Inicialización USB / BIOS Exception
        if "NO/INCORRECT USB CONFIG FOUND" in log_upper or re.search(r"EXCEPTION\s*#\d+", log_upper):
            exception_match = re.search(r"EXCEPTION\s*#(\d+)", log_upper)
            exc_num = exception_match.group(1) if exception_match else "?"
            
            return {
                "mode": "USB_CONFIG_ERROR",
                "status": "FAIL",
                "diagnosis": f"Falla de inicialización de hardware (USB/PCIe). El BIOS lanzó una excepción (EXCEPTION #{exc_num}).",
                "action": "Revisar falso contacto en el socket LGA (pines USB/PCIe). Verificar el estado del chipset/HUB de la AB."
            }
 
        # Si es PASS (llegó al prompt de Linux), lo sabemos aquí
        if "HARMAN-SI-INTEL-PLK" in log_upper:
            return {
                "mode": "NONE",
                "status": "PASS",
                "diagnosis": "Arranque de la AB exitoso. Sistema de memoria y alimentación OK.",
                "action": "Ninguna."
            }
 
        # Si el log no dice nada concluyente, el Filtro 1 no puede decidir.
        return {
            "mode": "UNKNOWN",
            "status": "UNKNOWN",
            "diagnosis": "El log no contiene información suficiente para diagnosticar la falla.",
            "action": "Requiere análisis con LEDs."
        }
 
    @staticmethod
    def analyze_with_leds(log_text: str, led_d62: str, led_d65: str) -> dict:
        """
        FILTRO 2: Se usa solo si el log no fue concluyente. Analiza los LEDs.
        """
        # Modo 04 por LEDs (Se apagaron por completo)
        if led_d62 == "LOW" and led_d65 == "LOW":
            return {
                "mode": "04",
                "status": "FAIL",
                "diagnosis": "Daño en el procesador Intel (D62 LOW, D65 LOW). El sistema se apagó después del DID.",
                "action": "Reemplazar el procesador Intel (IC10500)."
            }
 
        # Modo 01: Falla de PMIC (D62 High, D65 Low)
        if led_d62 == "HIGH" and led_d65 == "LOW":
            return {
                "mode": "01",
                "status": "FAIL",
                "diagnosis": "Falla de alimentación (PMIC). D62 HIGH, D65 LOW. La placa enciende pero no hay comunicación RH850.",
                "action": "Revisar voltajes (VDDQ, VCC). Reemplazar PMIC (IC10750)."
            }
        
        # Modo 02: Falla de Regulador (D62 High, D65 High)
        elif led_d62 == "HIGH" and led_d65 == "HIGH":
            return {
                "mode": "02",
                "status": "FAIL",
                "diagnosis": "Falla de comunicación con RH850. D62 HIGH, D65 HIGH.",
                "action": "Revisar Regulador (IC10851) y líneas de comunicación del procesador."
            }
        
        # Si el operador seleccionó cosas que no tienen sentido
        return {
            "mode": "UNKNOWN",
            "status": "UNKNOWN",
            "diagnosis": "No se pudo identificar el error con los datos actuales.",
            "action": "Requiere análisis manual."
        }
 

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\services\parser_service.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Servicio de parsing de resultados - VERSIÓN COMPLETA"""

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any


@dataclass
class ParsedResult:
    """Resultado parseado de una prueba"""
    status: str
    raw_value: str = ""
    data: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    metrics: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    
    @property
    def is_pass(self) -> bool:
        return self.status == "PASS"
    
    @property
    def is_fail(self) -> bool:
        return self.status == "FAIL"


class ParserService:
    """Servicio de parsing de resultados de pruebas - VERSIÓN COMPLETA"""
    
    RESULT_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\*(.*?)&")
    
    def parse(self, text: str, test_name: str) -> ParsedResult | None:
        """Parsea el resultado de una prueba"""
        result_match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
        raw_value = result_match.group(1) if result_match else text[:200]
        
        # ============================================================
        # VERSIONES
        # ============================================================
        if "CBSW Version" in test_name:
            match = re.search(r"v\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+\s+\d+", text)
            if match:
                return ParsedResult(status="CBSW", raw_value=raw_value, data={'version': match.group(0)}, metrics={'version': match.group(0)})
        
        if "ABHW Version" in test_name:
            match = re.search(r"ab ver:([A-Z])", text, re.IGNORECASE)
            if match:
                return ParsedResult(status="ABHW", raw_value=raw_value, data={'version': match.group(1)}, metrics={'version': match.group(1)})
        
        if "CBHW Version" in test_name:
            match = re.search(r"cb ver:([A-Z])", text, re.IGNORECASE)
            if match:
                return ParsedResult(status="CBHW", raw_value=raw_value, data={'version': match.group(1)}, metrics={'version': match.group(1)})
        
        # ============================================================
        # ADC
        # ============================================================
        if "ADC" in test_name:
            return self._parse_adc(text)
        
        # ============================================================
        # USB / PCIe IPERF
        # ============================================================
        if "USB3" in test_name and "number of port" not in test_name:
            parsed = self._parse_pcie(text)
            if parsed:
                return ParsedResult(status="USB3", raw_value=raw_value, data={'ports': parsed['ports']}, metrics={'ports': len(parsed['ports'])})
        
        if "USB2 Iperf Test" in test_name:
            parsed = self._parse_pcie(text)
            if parsed:
                return ParsedResult(status="USB2", raw_value=raw_value, data={'ports': parsed['ports']}, metrics={'ports': len(parsed['ports'])})
        
        if "PCIe Iperf Test" in test_name:
            parsed = self._parse_pcie(text)
            if parsed:
                return ParsedResult(status="PCIE", raw_value=raw_value, data={'ports': parsed['ports']}, metrics={'ports': len(parsed['ports'])})
        
        # ============================================================
        #  NUMBER OF PORT
        # ============================================================
        if test_name in [
            "USB3_3 number of port",
            "USB2 number of port",
            "PCIe number of port",
            "USB3_2 number of port"
        ]:
            # 1. Contamos la evidencia real de los puertos
            # Buscamos los registros exitosos en la sección [Register status]
            # o en el volcado de lsusb.
            
            registered_ports = 0
            
            if "PCIe" in test_name:
                # Para PCIe contamos las interfaces "Finally Ethernet interface name"
                registered_ports = text.count("Finally Ethernet interface name")
            elif "USB3" in test_name:
                # Para USB3 contamos los registros de ax88179_178a
                registered_ports = text.count("register 'ax88179_178a'")
            elif "USB2" in test_name:
                # Para USB2 contamos los registros de smsc95xx
                registered_ports = text.count("register 'smsc95xx'")
 
            # 2. Regla de oro: Si hay 4 o más registros reales, es un PASS
            # (Ignoramos el "Lost" porque el hardware está ahí)
            if registered_ports >= 4:
                return ParsedResult(
                    status="COMMAND ACCEPT",
                    raw_value=raw_value,
                    data={'message': f'{registered_ports} puertos detectados y registrados correctamente'}
                )
 
            # 3. Si NO hay 4 registros, entonces SÍ es un fallo real.
            # Intentamos leer cuántos puertos perdidos reporta el script.
            lost_match = re.search(r"Lost\s+(\d+)\s+ports?", text, re.IGNORECASE)
            lost_count = int(lost_match.group(1)) if lost_match else (4 - registered_ports)
            
            return ParsedResult(
                status="FAIL",
                raw_value=raw_value,
                data={'message': f'{lost_count} puertos perdidos', 'lost_ports': lost_count},
                metrics={'lost_ports': lost_count}
            )
 
        # ============================================================
        # USB3 <-> USB2 SWITCH
        # ============================================================
        if test_name in ["USB3 to USB2", "USB2 to USB3"]:
            return ParsedResult(status="CONFIGURATION", raw_value=raw_value, data={"message": "CONFIGURATION"})
        
        # ============================================================
        # UART
        # ============================================================
        if "UART" in test_name:
            uart_result = self._parse_uart(text)
            if uart_result:
                return uart_result
        
        # ============================================================
        # DDR / THERMAL / CURRENT / SMBUS / KERNEL / CPU INFO
        # ============================================================
        if "DDR" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 2:
                    return ParsedResult(status="DDR", raw_value=raw_value, data={'score': values[0], 'result': values[1]}, metrics={'score': values[0]})
        
        if "Thermal" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 2:
                    return ParsedResult(status="THERMAL", raw_value=raw_value, data={'temperature': values[0], 'humidity': values[1]}, metrics={'temp_c': float(values[0]), 'humidity_pct': float(values[1])})
        
        if "cursens" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 2:
                    return ParsedResult(status="CURRENT", raw_value=raw_value, data={'current': values[0], 'voltage': values[1]}, metrics={'current_a': float(values[0]), 'voltage_v': float(values[1])})
        
        if "SMBus" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 3:
                    return ParsedResult(status="SMBUS", raw_value=raw_value, data={'result': values[0], 'temp_c': values[1], 'temp_f': values[2]}, metrics={'temp_c': float(values[1]), 'temp_f': float(values[2])})
        
        if "Kernel Version" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 2:
                    return ParsedResult(status="KERNEL", raw_value=raw_value, data={'version': values[0], 'build_date': values[1]}, metrics={'version': values[0]})
        
        if "CPU info" in test_name:
            values = raw_value.split("|")
            if len(values) >= 13:
                return ParsedResult(status="CPUINFO", raw_value=raw_value, data={
                    "vendor_id": values[0], "cpu_family": values[1], "model": values[2],
                    "model_name": values[3], "cpu_cores": values[4], "cpu_mhz": values[5],
                    "cpu_max_mhz": values[6], "cpu_min_mhz": values[7], "bogomips": values[8],
                    "virtualization": values[9], "l1d_cache": values[10], "l1i_cache": values[11], "l2_cache": values[12]
                })
        
        # ============================================================
        # EMMC / SDIO
        # ============================================================
        if test_name in ["EMMC-8bit", "EMMC-4bit", "SDIO"]:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 4:
                    return ParsedResult(status="MMC", raw_value=raw_value, data={
                        'result': values[0], 'bus_width': values[1], 'clock': values[2], 'timing': values[3]
                    }, metrics={'bus_width': values[1], 'clock': values[2], 'timing': values[3]})
        
        # ============================================================
        # I2C / SPI / I2S
        # ============================================================
        if "I2C" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 2:
                    sensor_match = re.search(r"I2C_Temperature_(\d+)", text, re.IGNORECASE)
                    sensor = int(sensor_match.group(1)) if sensor_match else -1
                    return ParsedResult(status="I2C", raw_value=raw_value, data={"sensor": sensor, "temp_c": values[0], "temp_f": values[1]}, metrics={"sensor": sensor, "temp_c": float(values[0]), "temp_f": float(values[1])})
        
        if "SPI" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|", 1)
                if len(values) == 2:
                    return ParsedResult(status="SPI", raw_value=raw_value, data={'result': values[0], 'device_id': values[1]}, metrics={'result': values[0]})
        
        if "I2S" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                values = match.group(1).strip().split("|")
                if len(values) == 2:
                    return ParsedResult(status="I2S", raw_value=raw_value, data={'result': values[0], 'score': values[1]}, metrics={'score': values[1]})
        
        # ============================================================
        # PWM / MRC / HDMI / GPIO
        # ============================================================
        if test_name == "PWM":
            return ParsedResult(status="CONFIGURATION", raw_value=raw_value, data={'message': 'CONFIGURATION'})
        
        if "AB Mrcthreshold" in test_name:
            match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
            if match:
                return ParsedResult(status="MRCTHRESHOLD", raw_value=raw_value, data={'value': match.group(1).strip()})
        
        if test_name == "HDMI":
            return ParsedResult(status="COMMAND_ACCEPT", raw_value=raw_value, data={'message': 'COMMAND_ACCEPT'})
        
        if "GPIO" in test_name:
            return self._parse_gpio(text)
        
        # ============================================================
        # PARSEO GENÉRICO
        # ============================================================
        if result_match:
            value = result_match.group(1).strip()
            if value == "PASS":
                return ParsedResult(status="PASS", raw_value=raw_value)
            if value == "NG":
                return ParsedResult(status="FAIL", raw_value=raw_value)
            return ParsedResult(status="INFO", raw_value=raw_value, data={'value': value})
        
        if "*PASS&" in text:
            return ParsedResult(status="PASS", raw_value=raw_value)
        if "*NG&" in text:
            return ParsedResult(status="FAIL", raw_value=raw_value)
        if "ERASE DID/PDR SUCCESS" in text:
            return ParsedResult(status="PASS", raw_value=raw_value, data={'message': 'ERASE DID/PDR SUCCESS'})
        
        return None
    
    def _parse_adc(self, text: str) -> ParsedResult | None:
        channels = []
        for line in text.splitlines():
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue
            try:
                float(line)
                channels.append(line)
            except ValueError:
                if "DON'T CARE" in line:
                    channels.append(line)
        
        if channels:
            return ParsedResult(status="ADC", raw_value="|".join(channels), data={'channels': channels}, metrics={'channels': len(channels)})
        return None
    
    def _parse_pcie(self, text: str) -> dict | None:
        lines = []
        for line in text.splitlines():
            if "|PINGOK|" in line or "|PINGNG|" in line:
                lines.append(line)
        
        if not lines:
            return None
        
        compact = lines[0]
        fields = compact.split("|")
        ports = []
        idx = 1
        i = 1
        
        while i + 2 < len(fields):
            ping = fields[i].strip()
            tx = fields[i + 1].strip()
            rx = fields[i + 2].strip()
            if ping in ["PINGOK", "PINGNG"]:
                ports.append({"port": idx, "ping": ping, "tx": tx, "rx": rx})
                idx += 1
            i += 3
        
        return {"ports": ports} if ports else None
    
    def _parse_uart(self, text: str) -> ParsedResult | None:
        if "are identical" not in text:
            return None
        match = re.search(r"UART_rx_(\d+)", text)
        port = match.group(1) if match else "?"
        return ParsedResult(status="UART", raw_value=text[:200], data={'port': port, 'result': 'PASS'}, metrics={'port': port})
    
    def _parse_gpio(self, text: str) -> ParsedResult | None:
        match = self.RESULT_PATTERN.search(text)
        if not match:
            return None
        bits = match.group(1).replace("|", "")
        return ParsedResult(status="GPIO", raw_value=bits, data={'bits': bits}, metrics={'bits': bits})

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\services\sapme_client.py

import requests
import urllib3
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from pathlib import Path

urllib3.disable_warnings()

USERNAME = "report_user"
PASSWORD = "harman123"

SFC = "420036333048040404062289"

BASE_URL = "https://sapmii-mxqt.harman.com:51501"


class SapMeClient:

    def __init__(self):
        self.session = requests.Session()

    def login(self):

        print("[1] Opening login page...")

        login_page_url = (
            f"{BASE_URL}/XMII/Illuminator?service=Personalization"
        )

        response = self.session.get(
            login_page_url,
            verify=False,
            timeout=30
        )

        print(f"Login Page Status: {response.status_code}")

        soup = BeautifulSoup(
            response.text,
            "html.parser"
        )

        salt_input = soup.find(
            "input",
            {"name": "j_salt"}
        )

        if not salt_input:
            raise Exception(
                "Could not find j_salt field"
            )

        j_salt = salt_input["value"]

        print(f"j_salt = {j_salt}")

        login_url = (
            f"{BASE_URL}/XMII/j_security_check"
            "?service=Personalization"
        )

        payload = {
            "j_salt": j_salt,
            "j_username": USERNAME,
            "j_password": PASSWORD,
            "uidPasswordLogon": "Log On"
        }

        print("[2] Logging in...")

        login_response = self.session.post(
            login_url,
            data=payload,
            verify=False,
            allow_redirects=True,
            timeout=30
        )

        print(
            f"Login Status: {login_response.status_code}"
        )

        print(
            f"Final URL: {login_response.url}"
        )

        print("\nCookies:")

        for cookie in self.session.cookies:
            print(f"  {cookie.name}")

        Path("output").mkdir(
            exist_ok=True
        )

        with open(
            "output/login_response.html",
            "w",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as f:
            f.write(login_response.text)

        return login_response
    
    def get_sfc_status(self, sfc):

        print("\n[STATUS] Calling getSFCStatus...")

        url = (
            f"{BASE_URL}"
            "/XMII/Runner"
            "?transaction=REPORTING15/lifecycle/getSFCStatus"
            f"&in_sfc={sfc}"
            "&OutputParameter=xml_output"
            "&in_user=report_user"
        )

        response = self.session.get(
            url,
            verify=False,
            timeout=60
        )

        print(f"STATUS: {response.status_code}")

        with open(
            "sfc_status.xml",
            "w",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as f:
            f.write(response.text)

        return response.text
    def get_sfc_asbuild(self, sfc):

        print("\n[ASBUILD] Calling getSfcAsBuild...")

        url = (
            f"{BASE_URL}"
            "/XMII/Runner"
            "?transaction=REPORTING15/lifecycle/getSfcAsBuild"
            f"&in_sfc={sfc}"
            "&OutputParameter=xml_output"
            "&in_user=report_user"
        )

        response = self.session.get(
            url,
            verify=False,

        )

        print(

        )

        with open(

            "w",

        ) as f:

        return response.text
    def get_fct_page(self, sfc):

        print("\n[3] Loading FCT report...")

        url = (
            f"{BASE_URL}"
            "/XMII/CM/REPORTING15/"
            f"dc_non_ws.irpt?"
            f"sfc={sfc}"
            "&op=OP_FUNC_PCBA"
        )

        response = self.session.get(
            url,
            verify=False,
            timeout=30
        )
        print(response.url)
        print(response.history)
        print(
            f"FCT Status: {response.status_code}"
        )

        print(
            f"FCT URL: {response.url}"
        )

        with open(
            "output/fct_page.html",
            "w",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as f:
            f.write(response.text)
        with open(
            "fct_page_full.html",
            "w",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as f:
            f.write(response.text)
        return response

    def get_report_js(self):

        print("\n[4] Downloading JS...")

        js_url = (
            f"{BASE_URL}"
            "/XMII/CM/REPORTING15/"
            "scripts/dc_non_ws.js"
        )

        response = self.session.get(
            js_url,
            verify=False,
            timeout=30
        )

        print(
            f"JS Status: {response.status_code}"
        )

        with open(
            "output/dc_non_ws.js",
            "w",
            encoding="utf-8"
        ) as f:
            f.write(response.text)

        return response.text

    def get_parametrics(self, sfc):

        print("\n[5] Calling getParametricsBySFC...")

        runner_url = (
            f"{BASE_URL}"
            "/XMII/Runner"
            "?transaction=REPORTING15/lifecycle/getParametricsBySFC"
            f"&in_sfc={sfc}"
            "&in_op=OP_FUNC_PCBA"
            "&OutputParameter=xml_output"
            "&in_user=report_user"
        )

        print("\nURL:")
        print(runner_url)

        response = self.session.get(
            runner_url,
            verify=False,
            timeout=60
        )

        print(
            f"\nParametrics Status: {response.status_code}"
        )

        if response.status_code != 200:
                raise Exception(
                    f"SAPME error: {response.status_code}"
                )

        #print("\nFirst 3000 chars:\n")
        #print(response.text[:3000])

        return response.text

    def search_ajax_calls(self, js_text):

        print("\n[6] Looking for AJAX endpoints...")

        keywords = [
            "$.ajax",
            "$.get",
            "$.post",
            "ajax",
            "url:",
            "dataTable",
            "dcTable",
            "getParametricsBySFC"
        ]

        for key in keywords:

            if key.lower() in js_text.lower():

                print(
                    f"FOUND => {key}"
                )

        print(
            "\nSearch complete."
        )


def main():

    client = SapMeClient()

    client.login()

    fct = client.get_fct_page(
        SFC
    )
    print("\nFIRST 5000 CHARS OF FCT PAGE\n")
    print(fct.text[:5000])
    print(
        "\ndcTable Found:",
        "dcTable" in fct.text
    )

    print(
        "WORKING CURRENT Found:",
        "WORKING CURRENT" in fct.text
    )

    print(
        "TEST STEP DESCRIPTION Found:",
        "TEST STEP DESCRIPTION" in fct.text
    )

    js = client.get_report_js()

    client.search_ajax_calls(js)

    client.get_parametrics(
        SFC
    )

    print(
        "\nFiles generated:"
    )

    print(
        "output/login_response.html"
    )

    print(
        "output/fct_page.html"
    )

    print(
        "output/dc_non_ws.js"
    )

    print(
        "output/parametrics.xml"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3

import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

from bs4 import BeautifulSoup


def convert_value(value):

    if value is None:
        return None

    value = value.strip()

    #
    # GPIO bit patterns
    # Ejemplos:
    # 1111111111111101
    # 0000010000
    # 0111111111111111
    #
    if (
        len(value) >= 4
        and
        set(value).issubset(
            {"0", "1"}
        )
    ):
        return value

    #
    # Version strings
    #
    if "VER:" in value.upper():
        return value

    try:

        return float(
            value.replace(
                ",",
                "."
            )
        )

    except Exception:

        return value


def get_group(test_name):

    name = test_name.upper()

    if name.startswith("ADC0_") or name.startswith("ADC1_"):
        return "ADC"

    if name.startswith("ADC PORT"):
        return "ADC_DUPLICATE"

    if "PCIE" in name:
        return "PCIE"

    if "USB3_3" in name:
        return "USB3_3"

    if "USB3_2" in name:
        return "USB3_2"

    if "USB2" in name:
        return "USB2"

    if "CURSENS" in name:
        return "POWER"

    if "WORKING CURRENT" in name:
        return "POWER"

    if "TEMPERATURE" in name:
        return "THERMAL"

    if "HUMIDITY" in name:
        return "THERMAL"

    if "CB_BOARD_TEMP" in name:
        return "THERMAL"

    if "CPU" in name:
        return "CPU"

    if "KERNEL" in name:
        return "CPU"

    if "MODEL" in name:
        return "SYSTEM"

    if "VENDOR_ID" in name:
        return "SYSTEM"

    if "CACHE" in name:
        return "SYSTEM"

    if "BOGOMIPS" in name:
        return "SYSTEM"

    if "GROUP1_" in name:
        return "GPIO"

    if "GROUP2_" in name:
        return "GPIO"

    if "GROUP3_" in name:
        return "GPIO"

    if "SPI" in name:
        return "SPI"

    if "I2S" in name:
        return "I2S"

    if "URAT" in name or "UART" in name:
        return "UART"

    if "EMMC" in name:
        return "EMMC"

    if "CSI" in name:
        return "CSI"

    if "VERSION" in name:
        return "VERSION"

    if "VER:" in name:
        return "VERSION"

    return "MISC"


def normalize_pcie(group):

    result = {}

    for name, data in group.items():

        for port in range(4):

            if f"PCIE_{port}" in name:

                port_name = f"PORT_{port}"

                if port_name not in result:
                    result[port_name] = {}

                if "PING" in name:
                    result[port_name]["PING"] = data

                elif "TX" in name:
                    result[port_name]["TX"] = data

                elif "RX" in name:
                    result[port_name]["RX"] = data

    return result


def normalize_usb(group):

    result = {}

    for name, data in group.items():

        match = re.search(
            r'_(\d+)_(PING|TX|RX)',
            name
        )

        if not match:
            continue

        port = match.group(1)

        io_type = match.group(2)

        port_name = f"PORT_{port}"

        if port_name not in result:
            result[port_name] = {}

        result[port_name][io_type] = data

    return result


def is_version_field(test_name):

    test_name = test_name.upper()

    version_keywords = [
        "VERSION",
        "VER:",
        "FW_VERSION",
        "SW_VERSION",
        "KERNEL VERSION"
    ]

    return any(
        keyword in test_name
        for keyword in version_keywords
    )


def parse_parametrics(
    xml_text: str
):

    root = ET.fromstring(
        xml_text
    )

    data_node = root.find(
        "data"
    )

    if data_node is None:
        raise Exception(
            "No data node found"
        )

    html = data_node.text

    with open(
        "sapme_page.html",
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        f.write(html)
    soup = BeautifulSoup(
        html,
        "html.parser"
    )


    page_text = soup.get_text("\n")

 


    print("=" * 80)
    table = soup.find(
        "table",
        {"id": "dcTable"}
    )

    if table is None:
        raise Exception(
            "dcTable not found"
        )

    tbody = table.find(
        "tbody"
    )

    if tbody is None:
        raise Exception(
            "tbody not found"
        )

    grouped = {}

    rows = tbody.find_all(
        "tr"
    )

    for row in rows:

        cols = [
            td.get_text(strip=True)
            for td in row.find_all("td")
        ]

        if len(cols) < 13:
            continue
        material_rev = cols[2]
        test_step = cols[5]
        description = cols[6]
        status = cols[8]
        high_limit = cols[10]
        low_limit = cols[11]
        actual = cols[12]

        group = get_group(
            description
        )

        if group not in grouped:
            grouped[group] = {}

        if is_version_field(
            description
        ):

            high = str(
                high_limit
            ).strip()

            low = str(
                low_limit
            ).strip()

            actual_value = str(
                actual
            ).strip()

        else:

            high = convert_value(
                high_limit
            )

            low = convert_value(
                low_limit
            )

            actual_value = convert_value(
                actual
            )

        grouped[group][description] = {
            "test_step": test_step,
            "status": status,
            "high": high,
            "low": low,
            "actual": actual_value
        }

    #
    # Eliminar ADC duplicados
    #
    if "ADC_DUPLICATE" in grouped:
        del grouped["ADC_DUPLICATE"]

    #
    # Reorganizar PCIE
    #
    if "PCIE" in grouped:

        grouped["PCIE"] = normalize_pcie(
            grouped["PCIE"]
        )

    #
    # Reorganizar USB
    #
    if "USB2" in grouped:

        grouped["USB2"] = normalize_usb(
            grouped["USB2"]
        )

    if "USB3_2" in grouped:

        grouped["USB3_2"] = normalize_usb(
            grouped["USB3_2"]
        )

    if "USB3_3" in grouped:

        grouped["USB3_3"] = normalize_usb(
            grouped["USB3_3"]
        )
        grouped["_TRACEABILITY"] = {
            "material_rev": material_rev
        }
    return grouped

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from datetime import datetime

from core.services.sapme_client import SapMeClient
from core.services.sapme_parser import parse_parametrics
from core.repositories.project_repository import ProjectRepository
import re
from core.state.app_state import app_state

DEBUG = False
def debug_print(*args, **kwargs):

    if DEBUG:

        print(*args, **kwargs)
class SapmeService:

    def __init__(self):

        self.client = SapMeClient()

        self.project_repository = ProjectRepository()
    def reset(self):

        self.client = SapMeClient()
    def count_tests(
        self,
        limits: dict
    ):

        total = 0

        for group_data in limits.values():

            if isinstance(group_data, dict):

                total += len(group_data)

        return total

    def load_sfc(
        self,
        sfc: str
    ):

        #
        # Crear cliente nuevo para cada carga
        #
        self.client = SapMeClient()

        debug_print(
            f"Loading SAPME for {sfc}"
        )

        #
        # Login SAPME
        #
        self.client.login()

        #
        # Obtener Status (aquí viene ROUTING_REV)
        #
        status_xml = self.client.get_sfc_status(
            sfc
        )



        project = "UNKNOWN"

        routing_match = re.search(
            r'>([^<]*LGA[^<]*,\d+)<',
            status_xml
        )

        routing_rev = ""

        part_match = re.search(
            r'(\d{11}),\d{3}',
            status_xml
        )

        part_number = ""

        if part_match:

            part_number = part_match.group(1)
            if not part_number:

                print(
                    "ERROR: Part number not found"
                )

                return False
        print(
            f"PART NUMBER: {part_number}"
        )

        project = self.project_repository.get_project(
            part_number
        )

        if project == "UNKNOWN":

            print(
                "\n"
                + "=" * 80
            )

            print(
                f"ERROR: Unknown project for PN {part_number}"
            )

            print(
                "=" * 80
                + "\n"
            )

            app_state.sapme_ready = False

            return False

        print(
            f"PROJECT: {project}"
        )

        app_state.project = project

        xml_text = self.client.get_parametrics(
            sfc
        )


        #


        #
        # Descargar paramétricos
        #
        xml_text = self.client.get_parametrics(
            sfc
        )

        #
        # Parsear paramétricos
        #
        limits = parse_parametrics(
            xml_text
        )
        import json

        debug_print("\n========== SAPME LIMITS ==========\n")

        debug_print(
            json.dumps(
                limits,
                indent=4,
                default=str
            )
        )

        debug_print("\n===============================\n")
##
        traceability = limits.get(
            "_TRACEABILITY",
            {}
        )

        app_state.material_rev = (
            traceability.get(
                "material_rev",
                ""
            )
        )


        #
        # Actualizar AppState
        #
        app_state.current_sfc = sfc

        app_state.sapme_ready = True

        app_state.sapme_limits = limits

        app_state.operation = "OP_FUNC_PCBA"

        app_state.tests_loaded = self.count_tests(
            limits
        )

        app_state.last_sync = datetime.now()

        return True
