#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Servicio de comunicación serial"""

import logging
import time

from core.adapters.serial_adapter import SerialAdapter

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class SerialService:
    """Gestiona la comunicación serial con AB y CB"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.ab_adapter = SerialAdapter()
        self.cb_adapter = SerialAdapter()
        self._is_ready = False

    # ============================================================
    # CONEXIÓN DIRECTA
    # ============================================================

    def connect_cb(self, port: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        """Conecta a CB"""
        if not self.cb_adapter.connect(port):
            return False, f"Error conectando CB en {port}"

        board = self.cb_adapter.identify_board()
        if board != "CB":
            self.cb_adapter.disconnect()
            return False, f"Dispositivo no es CB (identificado como: {board})"

        return True, f"CB conectado en {port}"

    def connect_ab(self, port: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        """Conecta a AB"""
        if not self.ab_adapter.connect(port):
            return False, f"Error conectando AB en {port}"

        board = self.ab_adapter.identify_board()
        if board != "AB":
            self.ab_adapter.disconnect()
            return False, f"Dispositivo no es AB (identificado como: {board})"

        return True, f"AB conectado en {port}"

    # ============================================================
    # PREPARACIÓN
    # ============================================================

    def prepare_cb(self) -> bool:
        """Prepara CB para pruebas"""
        if not self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            return False
        self.cb_adapter.execute_command("hotkey_wssc.sh 0")
        return True

    def prepare_ab(self) -> bool:
        """Prepara AB para pruebas"""
        if not self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            return False
        self.ab_adapter.execute_command("hotkey_wssc.sh 0")
        return True

    # ============================================================
    # ENERGÍA
    # ============================================================

    def power_on_ab(self) -> bool:
        """Enciende AB"""
        if not self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            return False
        self.cb_adapter.execute_command("pwr_on_intl.sh 1")
        return True

    def power_off_ab(self) -> bool:
        """Apaga AB"""
        if not self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            return False
        self.ab_adapter.execute_command("websocket_test.sh powerswitch 0")
        return True

    def power_off_cb(self) -> bool:
        """Apaga CB"""
        if not self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            return False
        self.cb_adapter.execute_command("websocket_test.sh powerswitch 0")
        return True

    # ============================================================
    # ERASE DID
    # ============================================================

    def erase_did(self, timeout: int = 30) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        """Borra DID de AB"""
        if not self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            return False, "AB no conectado"

        response = self.ab_adapter.execute_command_until(
            "websocket_test.sh erasedid",
            "ERASE DID/PDR SUCCESS",
            timeout
        )

        if "ERASE DID/PDR SUCCESS" in response:
            return True, "DID borrado exitosamente"
        return False, f"Error borrando DID: {response[:200]}"

    # ============================================================
    # DESCONEXIÓN
    # ============================================================

    def disconnect_all(self):
        """Desconecta todos los dispositivos"""
        self.ab_adapter.disconnect()
        self.cb_adapter.disconnect()

    # ============================================================
    # PROPIEDADES
    # ============================================================

    @property
    def is_ready(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_ready

    def get_ports(self) -> list:
        """Obtiene TODOS los puertos disponibles (sin filtrar)"""
        return SerialAdapter.get_available_ports()

    def get_usb_serial_ports(self) -> list:
        """Obtiene solo puertos USB Serial (filtrados)"""
        return SerialAdapter.get_usb_serial_ports()

    # ============================================================
    # AUTO-DETECCIÓN
    # ============================================================

    def find_cb(self) -> str | None:
        """
        Busca CB automáticamente y CONECTA el adapter real.
        
        Itera sobre puertos USB Serial, prueba identificarlo como CB,
        y si lo encuentra, deja self.cb_adapter conectado a ese puerto.
        """
        ports = self.get_usb_serial_ports()

        print(f"[find_cb] Puertos USB Serial disponibles: {ports}")

        if not ports:
            print("[find_cb] ⚠️  No hay puertos USB Serial detectados")
            return None

        for port in ports:
            if (self.ab_adapter.is_connected()
                    and self.ab_adapter.port == port):
                continue

            if (self.cb_adapter.is_connected()
                    and self.cb_adapter.port == port):
                return port

            temp = SerialAdapter()

            try:
                if not temp.connect(port):
                    continue

                board = temp.identify_board()
                temp.disconnect()

                if board == "CB":
                    # ✅ CONECTAR EL ADAPTER REAL, no solo identificar
                    print(f"[find_cb] Identificado CB en {port}, conectando adapter...")
                    self.cb_adapter.connect(port)
                    print(f"[find_cb] ✅ CB encontrado en {port}")
                    return port

                print(f"[find_cb] {port} no es CB (detectado: {board})")

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[find_cb] Skip {port}: {e}")
                try:
                    temp.disconnect()
                except Exception:
                    pass

        print("[find_cb] ❌ CB no encontrado")
        return None


    def find_ab(self, timeout: int = 60) -> str | None:
        """
        Busca AB automáticamente y CONECTA el adapter real.
        """
        start = time.time()

        while (time.time() - start) < timeout:

            ports = self.get_usb_serial_ports()

            if ports:
                print(f"[find_ab] Puertos USB Serial disponibles: {ports}")

            for port in ports:
                if (self.cb_adapter.is_connected()
                        and self.cb_adapter.port == port):
                    continue

                if (self.ab_adapter.is_connected()
                        and self.ab_adapter.port == port):
                    return port

                temp = SerialAdapter()

                try:
                    if not temp.connect(port):
                        continue

                    board = temp.identify_board()
                    temp.disconnect()

                    if board == "AB":
                        # ✅ CONECTAR EL ADAPTER REAL
                        print(f"[find_ab] Identificado AB en {port}, conectando adapter...")
                        self.ab_adapter.connect(port)
                        print(f"[find_ab] ✅ AB encontrado en {port}")
                        return port

                    print(f"[find_ab] {port} no es AB (detectado: {board})")

                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[find_ab] Skip {port}: {e}")
                    try:
                        temp.disconnect()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            time.sleep(2)

        print("[find_ab] ❌ AB no encontrado")
        return None

    # ============================================================
    # LOGS
    # ============================================================

    def get_fixture_raw_log(self):
        """Obtiene logs crudos de ambos adaptadores"""
        log = []
        log.extend(self.cb_adapter.get_raw_log())
        log.extend(self.ab_adapter.get_raw_log())
        return log

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\services\test_service.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Servicio de ejecución de pruebas"""
 
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
 
from core.models.test_result import TestMetric, TestResult
from core.repositories.button_repository import ButtonRepository
from core.services.parser_service import ParserService
from core.services.serial_service import SerialService
from core.services.verification_service import VerificationService
 
 
class TestService:
    """Orquesta la ejecución de pruebas"""
    
    def __init__(self, serial_service: SerialService):
        self.serial_service = serial_service
        self.button_repo = ButtonRepository()
        self.parser = ParserService()
        self.verifier = VerificationService()
        self.on_progress: Callable | None = None
        self.on_log: Callable | None = None
    
    def set_callbacks(self, on_progress=None, on_log=None, on_error=None):
        """Configura callbacks (que deben ser señales)"""
        self._on_log = on_log
        self._on_progress = on_progress
        self._on_error = on_error
 
    def _log(self, message: str):
        """Emite mensaje de log (usa la señal)"""
        if self._on_log:
            try:
                self._on_log(message)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass
            
    def _progress(self, current: int, total: int):
        """Emite progreso"""
        if self.on_progress:
            self.on_progress(current, total)
    
    def execute_test(self, button: dict) -> TestResult:
        """Ejecuta una prueba individual"""
        label = button['label']
        action = button['action']
        test_type = action.get('type', 'single')
        
        self._log(f"▶ INICIANDO: {label}")
        
        start_time = time.time()
        result = TestResult(
            test_name=label,
            status="RUNNING",
            board_type="AB" if action.get('target') == 2 else "CB"
        )
        
        try:
            if test_type == 'single':
                result = self._execute_single(button, result)
            elif test_type == 'sequence':
                result = self._execute_sequence(button, result)
            elif test_type == 'sequence_buttons':
                result = self._execute_sequence_buttons(button, result)
            else:
                result.status = "FAIL"
                result.parsed_data = {'error': f'Tipo desconocido: {test_type}'}
        except Exception as e:
            result.status = "FAIL"
            result.parsed_data = {'error': str(e)}
            self._log(f" ERROR: {e!s}")
        
        result.duration_ms = (time.time() - start_time) * 1000
        self._log(f" FINALIZADO: {label} - {result.status}")
        
        return result
    
    def _execute_single(self, button: dict, result: TestResult) -> TestResult:
        """Ejecuta prueba tipo single"""
        action = button['action']
        cmd = action['cmd']
        target = action.get('target', 1)
        end_marker = action.get('end_marker')
        label = button['label']
        
        result.command = cmd
        
        # Seleccionar adaptador
        adapter = self.serial_service.cb_adapter if target == 1 else self.serial_service.ab_adapter
        
        # Ejecutar comando
        if end_marker:
            response = adapter.execute_command_until(cmd, end_marker, timeout=120)
        else:
            response = adapter.execute_command(cmd)
            print(f"[RESPONSE] {label} -> {response[:100]}")
            
        result.raw_response = response
        
        # Parsear
        parsed = self.parser.parse(response, label)
        result.parsed_data = parsed.__dict__ if parsed else {}
        
        # Verificar
        if parsed:
            verification = self.verifier.verify(label, parsed)
            result.verification = (verification.__dict__ if verification else {})
            result.status = (verification.status if verification else "UNKNOWN")
            
            if hasattr(verification, "metrics"):
                result.metrics = verification.metrics
        else:
            config_only_tests = {
                "DDR Set Test", "SATA Set port 1", "SATA Set Port 2",
                "USB3->USB2", "USB3_2", "PWM"
            }
            if label in config_only_tests:
                result.status = "PASS"
            else:
                result.status = "UNKNOWN"
        
        return result
 
    
    def _execute_sequence(self, button: dict, result: TestResult) -> TestResult:
        """Ejecuta prueba tipo sequence"""
        action = button['action']
        steps = action.get('steps', [])
 
        results = []
        current_gpio_pattern = None
        for step in steps:
            cmd = step.get('cmd')
            target = step.get('target', 1)
            end_marker = step.get('end_marker')
            delay = step.get('delay', 0)
 
            adapter = (self.serial_service.cb_adapter if target == 1 else self.serial_service.ab_adapter)
 
            if end_marker:
                response = adapter.execute_command_until(cmd, end_marker, timeout=120)
            else:
                response = adapter.execute_command(cmd)
 
            parsed = self.parser.parse(response, button['label'])
 
            # Detectar marker GPIO
            if parsed and parsed.status == "GPIO":
                bits = parsed.data.get("bits", "")
                if "GROUP" in bits:
                    current_gpio_pattern = bits
 
            # Asociar dump GPIO con el último marker
            if parsed and parsed.status == "GPIO" and current_gpio_pattern:
                parsed.data["pattern"] = current_gpio_pattern
                parsed.metrics["pattern"] = current_gpio_pattern
 
            verification = None
            if parsed:
                verification = self.verifier.verify(button["label"], parsed)
 
            results.append({
                "cmd": cmd, "response": response,
                "parsed": parsed, "verification": verification
            })
 
            if delay > 0:
                time.sleep(delay)
 
        result.raw_response = '\n'.join(r['response'] for r in results)
 
        serializable_steps = []
        for step in results:
            parsed = step.get("parsed")
            verification = step.get("verification")
            serializable_steps.append({
                "cmd": step.get("cmd", ""),
                "response": step.get("response", ""),
                "parsed": ({"status": parsed.status, "raw_value": parsed.raw_value, "data": parsed.data, "metrics": parsed.metrics} if parsed else {}),
                "verification": ({"status": verification.status, "message": verification.message, "details": verification.details} if verification else {})
            })
 
        result.parsed_data = {"steps": serializable_steps}
        
        if button["label"] == "ADC":
            for step in results:
                parsed = step.get("parsed")
                if parsed:
                    verification = self.verifier.verify("ADC", parsed)
 
        valid_status = {
            "PASS", "UART", "I2C", "SPI", "I2S", "GPIO", "ADC",
            "SMBUS", "MMC", "DDR", "THERMAL", "CURRENT",
            "CONFIGURATION", "COMMAND ACCEPT", "COMMAND_ACCEPT"
        }
 
        sequence_ok = True
        for step in results:
            response = step.get("response", "")
            if response == "NOT CONNECTED":
                sequence_ok = False
                break
            parsed = step.get("parsed")
            if parsed is None:
                continue
            verification = step.get("verification")
            if verification:
                if verification.status != "PASS":
                    sequence_ok = False
                    break
        
        result.status = "PASS" if sequence_ok else "FAIL"
        return result
    
    def _execute_sequence_buttons(self, button: dict, result: TestResult) -> TestResult:
        """Ejecuta secuencia de botones"""
        action = button['action']
        button_names = action.get('buttons', [])
        results = []
        total = len(button_names)
        
        for i, name in enumerate(button_names):
            self._progress(i + 1, total)
            self._log(f"📋 {i+1}/{total}: {name}")
            
            btn = self.button_repo.get_by_label(name)
            if not btn:
                self._log(f"⚠️ Botón no encontrado: {name}")
                continue
 
            sub_result = self.execute_test(btn)
            results.append(sub_result)
        
        result.parsed_data = {'sub_results': results}
        
        passed = sum(1 for r in results if r.status == "PASS")
        failed = len(results) - passed
        
        result.status = "PASS" if failed == 0 else "FAIL"
        result.metrics.append(TestMetric(name="total_tests", value=len(results), unit="count"))
        result.metrics.append(TestMetric(name="passed", value=passed, unit="count"))
        result.metrics.append(TestMetric(name="failed", value=failed, unit="count"))
        
        return result
 

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\services\verification_service.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Servicio de verificación de resultados - VERSIÓN COMPLETA"""

import logging
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from core.repositories.limits_repository import LimitsRepository
from core.services.parser_service import ParsedResult

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class VerificationResult:
    """Resultado de verificación"""
    status: str
    message: str = ""
    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    metrics: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def is_pass(self) -> bool:
        return self.status == "PASS"

    @property
    def is_fail(self) -> bool:
        return self.status == "FAIL"


class VerificationService:
    """Verifica resultados contra límites - VERSIÓN COMPLETA"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.limits_repo = LimitsRepository()

    def verify(self, test_name: str, parsed: ParsedResult | None) -> VerificationResult:
        print(f"[VERIFY]{test_name}")
        if not parsed:
            return VerificationResult(
                status="UNKNOWN",
                message="No hay datos para verificar"
            )
        # ----- NUMBER OF PORT -----
        if "number of port" in test_name.lower():
            if parsed.status == "FAIL":
                lost_ports = parsed.metrics.get("lost_ports", 0)
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"Falla de puertos: {lost_ports} puertos perdidos.",
                    details={'lost_ports': lost_ports}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message="Todos los puertos detectados correctamente."
            )
        
        if parsed.status in ["COMMAND ACCEPT", "COMMAND_ACCEPT", "CONFIGURATION"]:
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"Comando aceptado: {parsed.status}"
            )

        # ----- UART -----
        if "UART" in test_name:
            limits = self.limits_repo.get_limits(test_name)
            expected = "PASS"
            if limits:
                expected = limits.get("expected_result", "PASS")
            actual = parsed.data.get("result", "")
            if actual == expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="UART verificado correctamente"
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"UART esperado {expected}, obtenido {actual}"
            )

        # ----- SPI -----
        if "SPI" in test_name:
            if parsed.data.get('result', '') == "PASS":
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="SPI verificado correctamente",
                    details={'device_id': parsed.data.get('device_id', '')}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message="SPI falló"
            )

        # ----- I2S -----
        if "I2S" in test_name:
            limits = self.limits_repo.get_limits(test_name)
            expected = limits.get("expected_result", "PASS") if limits else "PASS"
            score_min = limits.get("score_min", 999) if limits else 999
            score_max = limits.get("score_max", 1001) if limits else 1001
            actual_result = parsed.data.get("result", "")
            score = int(parsed.metrics.get("score", 0))
            if actual_result == expected and score_min <= score <= score_max:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message=f"I2S OK score={score}",
                    details={"score": score}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"I2S FAIL result={actual_result} score={score}"
            )

        limits = self.limits_repo.get_limits(test_name)
        if not limits:
            return VerificationResult(
                status="UNKNOWN",
                message=f"No hay límites definidos para {test_name}"
            )

        # ----- CPU info -----
        if "CPU info" in test_name:
            checks = [
                ("vendor_id", "VENDOR_ID"),
                ("cpu_family", "CPU_FAMILY"),
                ("model", "MODEL"),
                ("model_name", "MODEL_NAME"),
                ("l1d_cache", "L1D_CACHE"),
                ("l1i_cache", "L1I_CACHE"),
                ("l2_cache", "L2_CACHE"),
            ]
            issues = []
            for field_key, label in checks:
                expected = str(limits.get(field_key, "")).strip().upper()
                actual = str(parsed.data.get(field_key, "")).strip().upper()
                expected = expected.replace(",", ".")
                actual = actual.replace(",", ".")
                if expected != actual:
                    issues.append(f"{label}: {actual} != {expected}")
            if issues:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="; ".join(issues)
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message="CPU INFO OK (SAPME)"
            )

        # ----- DDR -----
        if "DDR Get Result" in test_name:
            expected = limits.get("expected_status", "PASS")
            actual = parsed.data.get("result", "")
            score = float(parsed.data.get("score", 0))
            score_min = limits.get("score_min", -9999)
            score_max = limits.get("score_max", 9999)
            if actual != expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"CHECK DDR STATUS: {actual}",
                    details={"sapme_key": "CHECK DDR STATUS", "score": score}
                )
            if score < score_min or score > score_max:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"DDR VALUE: {score} fuera de rango [{score_min}-{score_max}]",
                    details={"sapme_key": "DDR VALUE", "score": score}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"CHECK DDR STATUS OK | DDR VALUE={score}",
                details={
                    "sapme_key": "DDR VALUE",
                    "status": actual,
                    "score": score,
                    "low": score_min,
                    "high": score_max
                }
            )

        # ----- THERMAL -----
        if "Thermal" in test_name:
            temp_min = limits.get('temperature_min', -100)
            temp_max = limits.get('temperature_max', 100)
            hum_min = limits.get('humidity_min', 0)
            hum_max = limits.get('humidity_max', 100)
            temp = parsed.metrics.get('temp_c', 0)
            hum = parsed.metrics.get('humidity_pct', 0)
            issues = []
            if temp < temp_min or temp > temp_max:
                issues.append(f"Temperatura {temp}°C fuera de rango [{temp_min}-{temp_max}]")
            if hum < hum_min or hum > hum_max:
                issues.append(f"Humedad {hum}% fuera de rango [{hum_min}-{hum_max}]")
            if issues:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="; ".join(issues),
                    details={'temperature': temp, 'humidity': hum}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"Thermal OK: {temp}°C, {hum}%",
                details={'temperature': temp, 'humidity': hum}
            )

        # ----- CURRENT -----
        if "cursens" in test_name:
            voltage = parsed.metrics.get("voltage_v", 0)
            current = parsed.metrics.get("current_a", 0)
            voltage_min = limits.get("voltage_min", 0)
            voltage_max = limits.get("voltage_max", 9999)
            current_min = limits.get("current_min", -999)
            current_max = limits.get("current_max", 999)
            issues = []
            if voltage < voltage_min or voltage > voltage_max:
                issues.append(f"Voltage {voltage}V")
            if not (current_min == -999 and current_max == 999):
                if current < current_min or current > current_max:
                    issues.append(f"Current {current}A")
            if issues:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="; ".join(issues),
                    details={"voltage": voltage, "current": current}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"Voltage OK: {voltage}V | Current OK: {current}A",
                details={
                    "voltage": voltage,
                    "current": current,
                    "voltage_low": voltage_min,
                    "voltage_high": voltage_max,
                    "current_low": current_min,
                    "current_high": current_max
                }
            )

        # ----- PCIe -----
        if "PCIe Iperf Test" in test_name:
            return self._verify_pcie(parsed, limits)

        # ----- USB3 -----
        if ("USB3_3 Iperf Test" in test_name or "USB3_2 Iperf Test" in test_name):
            return self._verify_usb3(parsed, limits)

        # ----- USB2 -----
        if "USB2 Iperf Test" in test_name:
            return self._verify_usb2(parsed, limits)

        # ----- EMMC/SDIO -----
        if test_name in ["EMMC-8bit", "EMMC-4bit", "SDIO"]:
            return self._verify_mmc(test_name, parsed, limits)

        # ----- GPIO (VERIFICACIÓN REAL BIT A BIT) -----
        if "GPIO" in test_name:
            patterns = {k: v for k, v in limits.items() if k.startswith("GROUP")}
            bits = parsed.data.get("bits", "")
            pattern_name = parsed.data.get("pattern", "")

            is_real_bits = (
                isinstance(bits, str)
                and len(bits) > 0
                and all(c in "01" for c in bits)
            )

            # 1. Ignorar comandos de configuración o errores de Linux
            if not is_real_bits:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="GPIO configuration step ignored",
                    details={
                        "pattern": pattern_name,
                        "marker": bits,
                        "patterns_count": len(patterns)
                    }
                )

            if not pattern_name:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"GPIO bits sin pattern_name: {bits}",
                    details={"bits": bits}
                )

            expected_data = patterns.get(pattern_name)

            # 2. Búsqueda inteligente (A prueba de typos como CABORDER)
            if expected_data is None:
                suffix = pattern_name.split("_")[-2:] 
                suffix_str = "_".join(suffix).replace(" ", "") if suffix else ""

                for key, val in patterns.items():
                    clean_key = key.replace("_", "").replace(" ", "").upper()
                    clean_pattern = pattern_name.replace("_", "").replace(" ", "").upper()
                    
                    if clean_key == clean_pattern or (suffix_str and suffix_str in key):
                        expected_data = val
                        break

            # 3. FALLBACK SEGURO: Si no existe en limits/SAPME, usamos el diccionario local
            if expected_data is None:
                fallback_patterns = {
                    "GROUP1_CB_ORDER HIGH_0-15": "1111111111111101",
                    "GROUP1_CB_ORDER LOW_0-15": "0000000000000000",
                    "GROUP1_AB_ORDER HIGH_0-15": "1111111111111101",
                    "GROUP1_AB_ORDER LOW_0-15": "0000000000000000",
                    "GROUP2_CB_ORDER HIGH_16-31": "0111111111111111",
                    "GROUP2_CB_ORDER LOW_16-31": "0000000000000000",
                    "GROUP2_AB_ORDER HIGH_16-31": "0111111111111111",
                    "GROUP2_AB_ORDER LOW_16-31": "0000000000000000",
                    "GROUP3_CB_ORDER HIGH_32-41": "1111111111",
                    "GROUP3_CB_ORDER LOW_32-41": "0000010000",
                    "GROUP3_AB_ORDER HIGH_32-41": "1111111111",
                    "GROUP3_AB_ORDER LOW_32-41": "0000010000",
                }

                expected_fallback = fallback_patterns.get(pattern_name)

                # Si ni siquiera está en el fallback, lo ignoramos (PASS)
                if expected_fallback is None:
                    return VerificationResult(
                        status="PASS",
                        message=f"GPIO pattern {pattern_name} no definido, ignorado",
                        details={"pattern": pattern_name, "bits": bits}
                    )

                
                if bits == expected_fallback:
                    return VerificationResult(
                        status="PASS",
                        message=f"GPIO OK (fallback): {pattern_name}",
                        details={
                            "pattern": pattern_name, 
                            "expected": expected_fallback, 
                            "actual": bits
                        }
                    )
                else:
                    return VerificationResult(
                        status="FAIL",
                        message=f"GPIO FAIL (fallback): {pattern_name} expected={expected_fallback} actual={bits}",
                        details={
                            "pattern": pattern_name, 
                            "expected": expected_fallback, 
                            "actual": bits
                        }
                    )

            # 4. Extraer el valor esperado real (de SAPME o limits.json)
            if isinstance(expected_data, dict):
                expected = str(expected_data.get("high", "")).strip()
            else:
                expected = str(expected_data).strip()

            if bits == expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message=f"GPIO OK: {pattern_name}",
                    details={
                        "pattern": pattern_name,
                        "expected": expected,
                        "actual": bits
                    }
                )

            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"GPIO FAIL: {pattern_name} expected={expected} actual={bits}",
                details={
                    "pattern": pattern_name,
                    "expected": expected,
                    "actual": bits
                }
            )
        # ----- ADC -----
        if "ADC" in test_name:
            channels = parsed.data.get("channels", [])
            if len(channels) < 6:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="ADC incompleto"
                )
            adc_limits = limits
            issues = []
            channel_results = []
            is_adc1 = False
            try:
                first_value = float(channels[0])
                if first_value < 1.2:
                    is_adc1 = True
            except Exception:
                pass
            prefix = "ADC1" if is_adc1 else "ADC0"
            max_channels = 6 if is_adc1 else 8
            for idx in range(max_channels):
                key = f"{prefix}_CH{idx}"
                if key not in adc_limits:
                    channel_results.append({
                        "channel": key,
                        "actual": None,
                        "low": None,
                        "high": None,
                        "result": "LIMIT NOT FOUND"
                    })
                    continue
                try:
                    value = float(channels[idx])
                except Exception:
                    channel_results.append({
                        "channel": key,
                        "actual": channels[idx],
                        "low": None,
                        "high": None,
                        "result": "INVALID"
                    })
                    issues.append(f"{key}: INVALID")
                    continue
                low = float(adc_limits[key]["low"])
                high = float(adc_limits[key]["high"])
                passed = low <= value <= high
                channel_results.append({
                    "channel": key,
                    "actual": value,
                    "low": low,
                    "high": high,
                    "result": "PASS" if passed else "FAIL"
                })
                if not passed:
                    issues.append(f"{key}: {value}")
            if issues:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="; ".join(issues),
                    details={"adc": prefix, "channels": channel_results}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"{prefix} OK (SAPME)",
                details={"adc": prefix, "channels": channel_results}
            )

        # ----- I2C -----
        if "I2C" in test_name:
            temp_min = limits.get("temperature_min", -100)
            temp_max = limits.get("temperature_max", 100)
            temp = parsed.metrics.get("temp_c", 0)
            sensor = parsed.data.get("sensor", "?")
            sapme_key = f"12C_TEMPERATURE_{sensor}"
            if temp < temp_min or temp > temp_max:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"{sapme_key}: {temp}°C fuera de rango [{temp_min}-{temp_max}]",
                    details={
                        "sapme_key": sapme_key,
                        "temp": temp,
                        "low": temp_min,
                        "high": temp_max
                    }
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"{sapme_key} OK: {temp}°C",
                details={
                    "sapme_key": sapme_key,
                    "temp": temp,
                    "low": temp_min,
                    "high": temp_max
                }
            )

        # ----- SMBus -----
        if "SMBus" in test_name:
            temp_min = limits.get("temperature_min", -100)
            temp_max = limits.get("temperature_max", 100)
            temp = parsed.metrics.get("temp_c", 0)
            if temp < temp_min or temp > temp_max:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"CHECK SMBUS: {temp}°C fuera de rango [{temp_min}-{temp_max}]",
                    details={
                        "sapme_key": "CHECK SMBUS",
                        "temp": temp,
                        "low": temp_min,
                        "high": temp_max
                    }
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"CHECK SMBUS OK: {temp}°C",
                details={
                    "sapme_key": "CHECK SMBUS",
                    "temp": temp,
                    "low": temp_min,
                    "high": temp_max
                }
            )

        # ----- LPC -----
        if "LPC" in test_name:
            expected = limits.get("expected_result", "PASS")
            actual = "PASS" if parsed.status == "PASS" else parsed.status
            if actual == expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="CHECK LPC STATUS OK"
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"CHECK LPC STATUS: esperado {expected}, obtenido {actual}"
            )

        # ----- AB Mrcthreshold -----
        if "AB Mrcthreshold" in test_name:
            value_str = parsed.data.get("value", "0")
            try:
                value = float(value_str.replace(",", "."))
            except Exception:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"Valor inválido: {value_str}"
                )
            value_min = limits.get("value_min", 0)
            value_max = limits.get("value_max", 9999)
            if value < value_min or value > value_max:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message=f"CHECK AB MRCTHRESHOLD: {value} fuera de rango [{value_min}-{value_max}]",
                    details={
                        "sapme_key": "CHECK AB MRCTHRESHOLD",
                        "value": value,
                        "low": value_min,
                        "high": value_max
                    }
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message=f"CHECK AB MRCTHRESHOLD OK: {value}",
                details={
                    "sapme_key": "CHECK AB MRCTHRESHOLD",
                    "value": value,
                    "low": value_min,
                    "high": value_max
                }
            )

        # ----- AB Erase DID -----
        if "AB Erase DID" in test_name:
            limits_local = self.limits_repo.get_limits(test_name)
            expected = ""
            if limits_local:
                expected = str(limits_local.get("expected_result", "")).strip().upper()
            actual = str(parsed.data.get("value", "")).strip().upper()
            if actual == "ERASE DID/PDR SUCCESS":
                actual = "PASSED"
            if actual == expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="CHECKERASEDIDRESULT OK",
                    details={"expected": expected, "actual": actual}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"Esperado {expected}, obtenido {actual}",
                details={"expected": expected, "actual": actual}
            )

        # ----- SATA -----
        if "SATA Get" in test_name:
            expected = limits.get("expected_result", "PASS")
            actual = "PASS" if parsed.status == "PASS" else "FAIL"
            if actual == expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message=f"{test_name} OK"
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"{test_name}: esperado {expected}, obtenido {actual}"
            )

        # ----- CSI (MENSAJE CON MOTIVO) -----
        if "CSI" in test_name:
            expected = limits.get("expected_result", "PASS")
            actual = "PASS" if parsed.status == "PASS" else "FAIL"
            if actual == expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="CSI verificado correctamente"
                )

            reason = ""
            raw = parsed.raw_value or ""

            if "Remote I/O error" in raw:
                m = re.search(r"Remote I/O error[^\r\n]*", raw)
                if m:
                    reason = m.group(0).strip()
            elif "diff: FAIL" in raw:
                reason = "diff: FAIL (patrón no coincide)"
            elif "*NG&" in raw:
                reason = "device respondió NG"

            message = f"CSI FAIL: {reason}" if reason else f"CSI esperado {expected}, obtenido {actual}"

            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=message,
                details={
                    "expected": expected,
                    "actual": actual,
                    "reason": reason
                }
            )

        # ----- AB Power Off (VERIFICACIÓN REAL) -----
        if "AB Power Off" in test_name:
            if not parsed:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="No hay datos de Power Off"
                )
            steps = parsed.data.get("steps", []) if isinstance(parsed.data, dict) else []
            if not steps:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="Sin steps en Power Off"
                )
            issues = []
            for step in steps:
                response = (step.get("response") or "").upper()
                if "ERROR" in response or "FAIL" in response:
                    issues.append(f"Step '{step.get('cmd', '?')}' reportó error")
                    continue
                if ("TERM SIGNAL" not in response
                        and "POWER DOWN" not in response
                        and "POWER OFF" not in response
                        and "LTC6904" not in response):
                    issues.append(f"Step '{step.get('cmd', '?')}' sin confirmación de apagado")
            if issues:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="FAIL",
                    message="; ".join(issues),
                    details={"issues": issues}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="PASS",
                message="Power Off verificado",
                details={"steps": len(steps)}
            )

 
        # ----- AB CPU TEMP -----
        if "AB CPU TEMP" in test_name or "AB CPU Temp" in test_name:
            temp = float(parsed.data.get("value", 0))
            temp_min = limits.get("temperature_min", 0)
            temp_max = limits.get("temperature_max", 100)
            if temp_min <= temp <= temp_max:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message=f"CPU Temp OK: {temp}°C"
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"CPU Temp fuera de rango: {temp}°C"
            )

        # ----- Verificación por valor esperado -----
        if "expected" in limits:
            expected = str(limits["expected"]).strip().upper()
            if "version" in parsed.data:
                actual = str(parsed.data["version"]).strip().upper()
            elif "value" in parsed.data:
                actual = str(parsed.data["value"]).strip().upper()
            else:
                actual = str(parsed.raw_value).strip().upper()
            if actual == expected:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="Valor esperado verificado",
                    details={"expected": expected, "actual": actual}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message=f"Esperado '{expected}' pero obtenido '{actual}'",
                details={"expected": expected, "actual": actual}
            )

        # ----- Type exists -----
        if limits.get('type') == 'exists':
            if parsed.raw_value:
                return VerificationResult(
                    status="PASS",
                    message="Datos encontrados",
                    details={'data': parsed.raw_value[:200]}
                )
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message="No se encontraron datos"
            )

        # ----- Fallback genérico -----
        if parsed.status == "PASS":
            return VerificationResult(status="PASS", message="Prueba PASS")
        if parsed.status == "FAIL":
            return VerificationResult(status="FAIL", message="Prueba FAIL")

        return VerificationResult(
            status=parsed.status,
            message="Verificación completada",
            details={'parsed_data': parsed.data}
        )

    # ============================================================
    # VERIFICADORES ESPECÍFICOS
    # ============================================================

    def _verify_pcie(self, parsed: ParsedResult, limits: dict) -> VerificationResult:
        ports = parsed.data.get("ports", [])
        sapme_ports = limits.get("pcie_limits", {})
        issues = []
        port_results = []
        port_mapping = {1: "PORT_0", 2: "PORT_1", 3: "PORT_2", 4: "PORT_3"}

        for port in ports:
            parser_port = port["port"]
            sapme_key = port_mapping.get(parser_port)
            if not sapme_key:
                continue
            if sapme_key not in sapme_ports:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: LIMIT NOT FOUND")
                continue
            expected = sapme_ports[sapme_key]
            expected_ping = str(expected["PING"]["high"])

            if expected_ping == "WEBSOCKET":
                port_results.append({
                    "port": sapme_key,
                    "rows": [
                        {"name": "PING", "low": "WEBSOCKET", "high": "WEBSOCKET",
                         "actual": port["ping"], "result": "N/A"},
                        {"name": "TX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": port["tx"], "result": "N/A"},
                        {"name": "RX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": port["rx"], "result": "N/A"}
                    ]
                })
                continue

            # PORT CON PING FALLIDO (agregar al detalle)
            if port["ping"] != expected_ping:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: {port['ping']} != {expected_ping}")
                port_results.append({
                    "port": sapme_key,
                    "rows": [
                        {"name": "PING", "low": expected_ping, "high": expected_ping,
                         "actual": port["ping"], "result": "FAIL"},
                        {"name": "TX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": port.get("tx", "NULL"), "result": "N/A"},
                        {"name": "RX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": port.get("rx", "NULL"), "result": "N/A"}
                    ]
                })
                continue

            if port["tx"] == "NULL" or port["rx"] == "NULL":
                port_results.append({
                    "port": sapme_key,
                    "rows": [
                        {"name": "PING", "low": expected_ping, "high": expected_ping,
                         "actual": port["ping"],
                         "result": "PASS" if port["ping"] == expected_ping else "FAIL"},
                        {"name": "TX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": "NULL", "result": "FAIL"},
                        {"name": "RX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": "NULL", "result": "FAIL"}
                    ]
                })
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: NULL RESULT")
                continue

            tx = float(port["tx"])
            rx = float(port["rx"])
            tx_min = float(expected["TX"]["low"])
            tx_max = float(expected["TX"]["high"])
            rx_min = float(expected["RX"]["low"])
            rx_max = float(expected["RX"]["high"])

            tx_result = "PASS"
            rx_result = "PASS"

            if not (tx_min == 0 and tx_max == 0):
                if tx < tx_min or tx > tx_max:
                    tx_result = "FAIL"
                    issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: TX {tx}")
            else:
                tx_result = "N/A"

            if not (rx_min == 0 and rx_max == 0):
                if rx < rx_min or rx > rx_max:
                    rx_result = "FAIL"
                    issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: RX {rx}")
            else:
                rx_result = "N/A"

            port_results.append({
                "port": sapme_key,
                "rows": [
                    {"name": "PING", "low": expected_ping, "high": expected_ping,
                     "actual": port["ping"],
                     "result": "PASS" if port["ping"] == expected_ping else "FAIL"},
                    {"name": "TX", "low": tx_min, "high": tx_max,
                     "actual": tx, "result": tx_result},
                    {"name": "RX", "low": rx_min, "high": rx_max,
                     "actual": rx, "result": rx_result}
                ]
            })

        if issues:
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message="; ".join(issues),
                details={"ports": port_results}
            )

        return VerificationResult(
            status="PASS",
            message=f"PCIe OK ({len(ports)} puertos SAPME)",
            details={"ports": port_results}
        )

    def _verify_usb3(self, parsed: ParsedResult, limits: dict) -> VerificationResult:
        ports = parsed.data.get("ports", [])
        sapme_ports = limits.get("usb_limits", {})
        issues = []
        port_results = []
        port_mapping = {1: "PORT_0", 2: "PORT_1", 3: "PORT_2", 4: "PORT_3"}

        for port in ports:
            parser_port = port["port"]
            sapme_key = port_mapping.get(parser_port)
            if not sapme_key:
                continue
            if sapme_key not in sapme_ports:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: LIMIT NOT FOUND")
                continue
            expected = sapme_ports[sapme_key]
            expected_ping = str(expected["PING"]["high"])

            # PORT CON PING FALLIDO (agregar al detalle)
            if port["ping"] != expected_ping:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: {port['ping']} != {expected_ping}")
                port_results.append({
                    "port": sapme_key,
                    "rows": [
                        {"name": "PING", "low": expected_ping, "high": expected_ping,
                         "actual": port["ping"], "result": "FAIL"},
                        {"name": "TX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": port.get("tx", "NULL"), "result": "N/A"},
                        {"name": "RX", "low": "N/A", "high": "N/A",
                         "actual": port.get("rx", "NULL"), "result": "N/A"}
                    ]
                })
                continue

            tx = float(port["tx"])
            rx = float(port["rx"])
            tx_min = float(expected["TX"]["low"])
            tx_max = float(expected["TX"]["high"])
            rx_min = float(expected["RX"]["low"])
            rx_max = float(expected["RX"]["high"])

            if not (tx_min == 0 and tx_max == 0):
                if tx < tx_min or tx > tx_max:
                    issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: TX {tx}")

            if not (rx_min == 0 and rx_max == 0):
                if rx < rx_min or rx > rx_max:
                    issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: RX {rx}")

            port_results.append({
                "port": sapme_key,
                "rows": [
                    {"name": "PING", "low": expected_ping, "high": expected_ping,
                     "actual": port["ping"],
                     "result": "PASS" if port["ping"] == expected_ping else "FAIL"},
                    {"name": "TX", "low": tx_min, "high": tx_max,
                     "actual": tx,
                     "result": "N/A" if (tx_min == 0 and tx_max == 0)
                     else ("PASS" if tx_min <= tx <= tx_max else "FAIL")},
                    {"name": "RX", "low": rx_min, "high": rx_max,
                     "actual": rx,
                     "result": "N/A" if (rx_min == 0 and rx_max == 0)
                     else ("PASS" if rx_min <= rx <= rx_max else "FAIL")}
                ]
            })

        if issues:
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message="; ".join(issues),
                details={"ports": port_results}
            )

        return VerificationResult(
            status="PASS",
            message="USB3 OK (SAPME)",
            details={"ports": port_results}
        )

    def _verify_usb2(self, parsed: ParsedResult, limits: dict) -> VerificationResult:
        ports = parsed.data.get("ports", [])
        sapme_ports = limits.get("usb_limits", {})
        issues = []
        port_mapping = {1: "PORT_0", 2: "PORT_1", 3: "PORT_2", 4: "PORT_3"}
        port_results = []

        for port in ports:
            parser_port = port["port"]
            sapme_key = port_mapping.get(parser_port)
            if not sapme_key:
                continue
            if sapme_key not in sapme_ports:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: LIMIT NOT FOUND")
                continue
            expected = sapme_ports[sapme_key]
            expected_ping = str(expected["PING"]["high"])

            tx = float(port["tx"])
            rx = float(port["rx"])
            tx_min = float(expected["TX"]["low"])
            tx_max = float(expected["TX"]["high"])
            rx_min = float(expected["RX"]["low"])
            rx_max = float(expected["RX"]["high"])

            # PORT CON PING FALLIDO (agregar al detalle)
            if port["ping"] != expected_ping:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: {port['ping']} != {expected_ping}")
                port_results.append({
                    "port": sapme_key,
                    "rows": [
                        {"name": "PING", "low": expected_ping, "high": expected_ping,
                         "actual": port["ping"], "result": "FAIL"},
                        {"name": "TX", "low": tx_min, "high": tx_max,
                         "actual": tx, "result": "N/A"},
                        {"name": "RX", "low": rx_min, "high": rx_max,
                         "actual": rx, "result": "N/A"}
                    ]
                })
                continue

            port_results.append({
                "port": sapme_key,
                "rows": [
                    {"name": "PING", "low": expected_ping, "high": expected_ping,
                     "actual": port["ping"],
                     "result": "PASS" if port["ping"] == expected_ping else "FAIL"},
                    {"name": "TX", "low": tx_min, "high": tx_max,
                     "actual": tx,
                     "result": "PASS" if tx_min <= tx <= tx_max else "FAIL"},
                    {"name": "RX", "low": rx_min, "high": rx_max,
                     "actual": rx,
                     "result": "PASS" if rx_min <= rx <= rx_max else "FAIL"}
                ]
            })

            if port["ping"] != expected_ping:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: {port['ping']} != {expected_ping}")
            if tx < tx_min or tx > tx_max:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: TX {tx}")
            if rx < rx_min or rx > rx_max:
                issues.append(f"{sapme_key}: RX {rx}")

        if issues:
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message="; ".join(issues),
                details={"ports": port_results}
            )

        return VerificationResult(
            status="PASS",
            message="USB2 OK (SAPME)",
            details={"ports": port_results}
        )

    def _verify_mmc(self, test_name: str, parsed: ParsedResult, limits: dict) -> VerificationResult:
        expected_bus_width = limits.get('bus_width')
        expected_clock = limits.get('clock')
        expected_timing = limits.get('timing')
        expected_result = limits.get('result')
        actual_bus_width = parsed.metrics.get('bus_width', '')
        actual_clock = parsed.metrics.get('clock', '')
        actual_timing = parsed.metrics.get('timing', '')
        actual_result = parsed.data.get('result', '')
        issues = []
        if expected_bus_width and actual_bus_width != expected_bus_width:
            issues.append(f"Bus width: {actual_bus_width} (esperado {expected_bus_width})")
        if expected_clock and actual_clock != expected_clock:
            issues.append(f"Clock: {actual_clock} (esperado {expected_clock})")
        if expected_timing and actual_timing != expected_timing:
            issues.append(f"Timing: {actual_timing} (esperado {expected_timing})")
        if expected_result is not None:
            if actual_result != expected_result:
                issues.append(f"Resultado: {actual_result} (esperado {expected_result})")
        if issues:
            return VerificationResult(
                status="FAIL",
                message="; ".join(issues),
                details={'issues': issues}
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status="PASS",
            message=f"{test_name} verificado correctamente"
        )

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from datetime import datetime


class AppState:

    def __init__(self):

        self.current_sfc = None

        self.project = ""

        self.sapme_ready = False

        self.sapme_limits = {}

        self.operation = None

        self.tests_loaded = 0

        self.last_sync = None

        self.source = "SAPME"

    def reset(self):

        self.current_sfc = None

        self.project = ""

        self.sapme_ready = False

        self.sapme_limits = {}

        self.operation = None

        self.tests_loaded = 0

        self.last_sync = None

        self.source = "SAPME"



app_state = AppState()

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from functools import partial

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGridLayout, QGroupBox, QScrollArea, QVBoxLayout, QWidget
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from config import dashboard_groups
from config.dashboard_groups import DASHBOARD_GROUPS
from core.ui.widgets.collapsible_group import CollapsibleGroup
from core.ui.widgets.test_card import TestCard


class DashboardBuilder:

    @staticmethod
    def filter_cards(
        search_text,
        dashboard_cards,
        dashboard_groups
    ):

        search_text = (
            search_text
            .strip()
            .lower()
        )

        #
        # Sin filtro
        #
        if not search_text:

            for card in dashboard_cards.values():

                card.show()

            for group in dashboard_groups.values():

                group.show()

                group.content.hide()

                group._expanded = False

                group.refresh_title()

            return

        #
        # Agrupar coincidencias por grupo
        #
        visible_groups = set()

        for group_name, group in dashboard_groups.items():

            group_has_match = False

            content_widget = (
                group.content_layout.itemAt(0).widget()
            )

            grid = content_widget.layout()

            for row in range(grid.count()):

                item = grid.itemAt(row)

                if not item:

                    continue

                card = item.widget()

                if not card:

                    continue

                test_name = (
                    card.test_name
                    if hasattr(card, "test_name")
                    else card.text()
                )

                if search_text in test_name.lower():

                    card.show()

                    group_has_match = True

                else:

                    card.hide()

            if group_has_match:

                group.show()

                #
                # expandir grupo
                #
                if not group._expanded:

                    group.toggle()

                visible_groups.add(
                    group_name
                )

            else:

                group.hide()
    def build(run_callback,view_result_callback):

        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)

        container = QWidget()
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(container)

        dashboard_cards = {}
        dashboard_groups = {}
        for group_name, tests in DASHBOARD_GROUPS.items():

            group_box = CollapsibleGroup(
                group_name
            )
  
            dashboard_groups[group_name] = group_box
            content_widget = QWidget()

            grid = QGridLayout(content_widget)
            content_widget.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                background-color: #F7F8FA;
                border: 1px solid #D8DCE3;
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            """)
            grid.setContentsMargins(
                12,
                12,
                12,
                12
            )

            group_box.content_layout.addWidget(
                content_widget
            )

            if group_name == "USB - PCIE":

                positions = {
                    "PCIe number of port": (0, 0),
                    "PCIe Iperf Test": (1, 0),

                    "USB3_3 number of port": (0, 1),
                    "USB3_3 Iperf Test": (1, 1),

                    "USB3_2 number of port": (2, 1),
                    "USB3_2 Iperf Test": (3, 1),

                    "USB2 number of port": (0, 2),
                    "USB2 Iperf Test": (1, 2),

                    "USB3 to USB2": (0, 3),
                    "USB2 to USB3": (1, 3),
                }

                for test_name in tests:

                    row, col = positions[test_name]

                    card = TestCard(test_name)

                    card.clicked.connect(
                        partial(
                            run_callback,
                            test_name
                        )
                    )

                    card.rightClicked.connect(
                        view_result_callback
                    )

                    grid.addWidget(
                        card,
                        row,
                        col
                    )

                    dashboard_cards[test_name] = card

            else:

                for idx, test_name in enumerate(tests):

                    row = idx // 4
                    col = idx % 4

                    card = TestCard(test_name)

                    card.clicked.connect(
                        partial(
                            run_callback,
                            test_name
                        )
                    )

                    card.rightClicked.connect(
                        view_result_callback
                    )

                    grid.addWidget(
                        card,
                        row,
                        col
                    )

                    dashboard_cards[test_name] = card

            main_layout.addWidget(
                group_box
            )

        main_layout.addStretch()

        scroll.setWidget(container)

        return (
            scroll,
            dashboard_cards,
            dashboard_groups
        )

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\ui\dashboard_view.py
================================================================================

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QPushButton,
    QWidget,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QProgressBar
)
from config import dashboard_groups
from core.ui.styles import AppStyles
from core.ui.widgets.fixture_control_widget import (
    FixtureControlWidget
)
from core.ui.dashboard_builder import DashboardBuilder
from controllers.dashboard_controller import DashboardController
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt


class DashboardView:

    @staticmethod
    def create(
        run_callback,
        run_all_callback,
        view_result_callback,
        test_controller,
        button_repo,
        log_callback,
        fixture_controller
    ):

        (
            dashboard_widget,
            dashboard_cards,
            dashboard_groups
        ) = DashboardBuilder.build(
            run_callback,
            view_result_callback
        )
        run_all_button = QPushButton(
            "RUN ALL TESTS"
        )
        run_all_button.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_ALL_BUTTON
        )
        run_all_button.setCursor(
            Qt.PointingHandCursor
        )
        run_all_button.clicked.connect(
            run_all_callback
        )
        # Resumen
        summary_label = QLabel(
            "✅ PASS: 0    ❌ FAIL: 0    ⏳ NOT RUN: 0"
        )

        summary_label.setStyleSheet("""
            font-size: 14px;
            font-weight: bold;
            padding: 6px;
        """)

        progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        fixture_widget = FixtureControlWidget(
            fixture_controller,
            run_all_button,
            lambda text: DashboardBuilder.filter_cards(
                text,
                dashboard_cards,
                dashboard_groups
            )
        )
        progress_bar.setMinimum(0)
        progress_bar.setMaximum(100)
        progress_bar.setValue(0)

        # Contenedor principal
        container = QWidget()

        layout = QVBoxLayout(container)
        
        layout.addWidget(fixture_widget)
#        layout.addWidget(summary_label)
#        layout.addWidget(progress_bar)
        layout.addWidget(dashboard_widget)
      

        # Controller
        controller = DashboardController(
            dashboard_cards,
            dashboard_groups,
            test_controller,
            button_repo,
            log_callback,
            summary_label,
            progress_bar
        )

        # Inicializar contadores
        controller.update_dashboard()

        return (
            container,
            dashboard_cards,
            dashboard_groups,
            controller,
            summary_label,
            progress_bar,
            fixture_widget,
            run_all_button
        )

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Vista de Analysis - Captura continua de logs AB/CB"""

import os
import time
import threading
from datetime import datetime
from core.services.diagnostic_engine import DiagnosticEngine
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QTimer, Signal, Qt
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QMessageBox,
    QWidget,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QPushButton,
    QComboBox,
    QTextBrowser,
    QGroupBox,
)

from core.adapters.serial_adapter import SerialAdapter
from core.ui.styles import AppStyles
from core.ui.widgets.led_selector_dialog import LedSelectorDialog
from core.ui.workers.fixture_worker import FixtureWorker


class LogSignals(QObject):
    """Señales para comunicación entre hilos de captura y UI."""
    cb_text = Signal(str)
    ab_text = Signal(str)


class AnalysisView(QWidget):
    """
    Vista de Analysis para diagnóstico AB/CB.
    
    Permite:
        - Conectar CB y AB por COM
        - Capturar logs continuos de ambos puertos
        - Guardar logs combinados
        - Ejecutar "AB CONNECTION" (power on AB)
    """

    def __init__(self,fixture_controller):
        super().__init__()

        # Inicializar controlador de fixture
        self.fixture_controller = (fixture_controller)
        # Adaptadores
        self.cb_adapter = SerialAdapter()
        self.ab_adapter = SerialAdapter()

        # Flags de captura
        self.cb_capture_running = False
        self.ab_capture_running = False

        # Buffers de log
        self.cb_log_buffer = []
        self.ab_log_buffer = []

        # Señales Qt (para actualizar UI desde hilos)
        self.signals = LogSignals()

        # Construir UI
        self._build_ui()

        # Conectar señales a consolas
        self.signals.cb_text.connect(self.cb_console.insertPlainText)
        self.signals.ab_text.connect(self.ab_console.insertPlainText)
        self.refresh_ports()
    # ============================================================
    # CONSTRUCCIÓN DE UI
    # ============================================================

    def _build_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        # --------------------------
        # ANALYSIS ACTIONS
        # --------------------------
        actions_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_refresh_ports = QPushButton(
            "REFRESH PORTS"
        )

        self.btn_refresh_ports.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_ALL_BUTTON
        )

        self.btn_refresh_ports.setFixedHeight(
            28
        )

        self.btn_ab_connection = QPushButton(
            "AB CONNECTION"
        )

        self.btn_ab_connection.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_ALL_BUTTON
        )

        self.btn_ab_connection.setFixedHeight(
            28
        )

        self.btn_clear_terminal = QPushButton(
            "CLEAR TERMINAL"
        )

        self.btn_clear_terminal.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_ALL_BUTTON
        )

        self.btn_clear_terminal.setFixedHeight(
            28
        )

        self.btn_save_log = QPushButton(
            "SAVE LOG"
        )

        self.btn_save_log.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_ALL_BUTTON
        )

        self.btn_save_log.setFixedHeight(
            28
        )

        self.btn_disconnect = QPushButton(
            "DISCONNECT"
        )

        self.btn_disconnect.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_ALL_BUTTON
        )

        self.btn_disconnect.setFixedHeight(
            28
        )

        actions_layout.addStretch()

        actions_layout.addWidget(
            self.btn_ab_connection
        )

        actions_layout.addWidget(
            self.btn_refresh_ports
        )

        actions_layout.addWidget(
            self.btn_clear_terminal
        )

        actions_layout.addWidget(
            self.btn_save_log
        )

        actions_layout.addWidget(
            self.btn_disconnect
        )

        layout.addLayout(

        )

        # --------------------------
        # CB BOARD
        # --------------------------
        self.cb_group = QGroupBox("CB BOARD")

        cb_layout.setSpacing(6)

        self.lbl_cb_status = QLabel("STATUS: DISCONNECTED")
        self.lbl_cb_status.setStyleSheet("""
            font-weight: bold;

        """)
        cb_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_cb_status)

        self.cb_combo.setMinimumWidth(200)

        self.btn_cb_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.ANALYSIS_BUTTON)
        cb_top.addWidget(self.btn_cb_connect)

        self.btn_cb_kill = QPushButton("KILL WSSC")
        self.btn_cb_kill.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.ANALYSIS_BUTTON)
        cb_top.addWidget(self.btn_cb_kill)

        self.btn_cb_power_off = QPushButton("POWER OFF")
        self.btn_cb_power_off.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.ANALYSIS_BUTTON)
        cb_top.addWidget(self.btn_cb_power_off)
        
        cb_top.addStretch()
        cb_layout.addLayout(cb_top)
