        self.cb_console.setPlaceholderText("CB console output...")
        cb_layout.addWidget(self.cb_console)

        layout.addWidget(self.cb_group)

        # --------------------------
        # AB BOARD
        # --------------------------
        self.ab_group = QGroupBox("AB BOARD")
        ab_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.ab_group)
        ab_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 8, 8, 8)
        ab_layout.setSpacing(6)

        self.lbl_ab_status = QLabel("STATUS: DISCONNECTED")
        self.lbl_ab_status.setStyleSheet("""
            font-weight: bold;
            color: #404040;
        """)
        ab_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_ab_status)

        ab_top = QHBoxLayout()

        ab_top.addWidget(QLabel("COM:"))

        self.ab_combo = QComboBox()
        self.ab_combo.setMinimumWidth(200)
        ab_top.addWidget(self.ab_combo)

        self.btn_ab_connect = QPushButton("CONNECT")
        self.btn_ab_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.ANALYSIS_BUTTON)
        ab_top.addWidget(self.btn_ab_connect)

        self.btn_ab_kill = QPushButton("KILL WSSC")
        self.btn_ab_kill.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.ANALYSIS_BUTTON)
        ab_top.addWidget(self.btn_ab_kill)

        ab_top.addStretch()
        ab_layout.addLayout(ab_top)

        self.ab_console = QTextBrowser()
        self.ab_console.setPlaceholderText("AB console output...")
        ab_layout.addWidget(self.ab_console)

        layout.addWidget(self.ab_group)

        layout.addStretch()

        # --------------------------
        # CONEXIONES
        # --------------------------
        self.btn_refresh_ports.clicked.connect(self.refresh_ports)
        self.btn_cb_connect.clicked.connect(self.connect_cb)
        self.btn_ab_connect.clicked.connect(self.connect_ab)
        self.btn_cb_kill.clicked.connect(self.kill_cb_wssc)
        self.btn_cb_power_off.clicked.connect(self.power_off_cb)
        self.btn_ab_kill.clicked.connect(self.kill_ab_wssc)
        self.btn_ab_connection.clicked.connect(self.start_ab_connection_analysis)
        self.btn_save_log.clicked.connect(self.save_log)
        self.btn_disconnect.clicked.connect(self.disconnect_all)
        self.btn_clear_terminal.clicked.connect(self.clear_terminal)

    # ============================================================
    # UTILIDADES
    # ============================================================

    def set_ports(self, ports):
        """Carga la lista de puertos COM en ambos combos."""
        self.cb_combo.clear()
        self.ab_combo.clear()
        self.cb_combo.addItems(ports)
        self.ab_combo.addItems(ports)

    def refresh_ports(self):
        """Refresca la lista de puertos disponibles."""
        ports = SerialAdapter.get_available_ports()
        self.set_ports(ports)
        self.cb_console.append(f"Available ports: {ports}")
        self.ab_console.append(f"Available ports: {ports}")

    # ============================================================
    # CONEXIÓN
    # ============================================================

    def connect_cb(self):
        """Conecta al puerto CB."""
        if self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            self.cb_console.append(f"Already connected to {self.cb_adapter.port}")
            return

        port = self.cb_combo.currentText()
        if not port:
            return

        ok = self.cb_adapter.connect(port)

        if ok:
            self.lbl_cb_status.setText("STATUS: CONNECTED")
            self.lbl_cb_status.setStyleSheet("""
                color: #16A34A;
                font-weight: 600;
            """)
            self.cb_console.append(f"Connected to {port}")
            self.start_cb_capture()
        else:
            self.lbl_cb_status.setText("STATUS: FAILED")
            self.lbl_cb_status.setStyleSheet("""
                color: #DC2626;
                font-weight: 600;
            """)
            self.cb_console.append(f"Failed to connect to {port}")

    def connect_ab(self):
        """Conecta al puerto AB."""
        if self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            self.ab_console.append(f"Already connected to {self.ab_adapter.port}")
            return

        port = self.ab_combo.currentText()
        if not port:
            return

        ok = self.ab_adapter.connect(port)

        if ok:
            self.lbl_ab_status.setText("STATUS: CONNECTED")
            self.lbl_ab_status.setStyleSheet("""
                color: #16A34A;
                font-weight: 600;
            """)
            self.ab_console.append(f"Connected to {port}")
            self.start_ab_capture()
        else:
            self.lbl_ab_status.setText("STATUS: FAILED")
            self.lbl_ab_status.setStyleSheet("""
                color: #DC2626;
                font-weight: 600;
            """)
            self.ab_console.append(f"Failed to connect to {port}")

    # ============================================================
    # CAPTURA CONTINUA
    # ============================================================

    def start_cb_capture(self):
        """Inicia captura continua del puerto CB."""
        if self.cb_capture_running:
            return

        self.cb_capture_running = True

        def worker():
            while self.cb_capture_running and self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
                try:
                    if self.cb_adapter._serial.in_waiting:
                        data = self.cb_adapter._serial.read(
                            self.cb_adapter._serial.in_waiting
                        ).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")

                        if data:
                            self.cb_log_buffer.append(data)
                            # Emitir por señal (thread-safe)
                            self.signals.cb_text.emit(data)
                    time.sleep(0.01)
                except Exception:
                    break

        threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()

    def start_ab_capture(self):
        """Inicia captura continua del puerto AB."""
        if self.ab_capture_running:
            return

        self.ab_capture_running = True

        def worker():
            while self.ab_capture_running and self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
                try:
                    if self.ab_adapter._serial.in_waiting:
                        data = self.ab_adapter._serial.read(
                            self.ab_adapter._serial.in_waiting
                        ).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")

                        if data:
                            self.ab_log_buffer.append(data)
                            # Emitir por señal (thread-safe)
                            self.signals.ab_text.emit(data)
                    time.sleep(0.01)
                except Exception:
                    break

        threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()

    # ============================================================
    # KILL WSSC
    # ============================================================

    def kill_cb_wssc(self):
        """Envía comando para matar WSSC en CB."""
        if not self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            return

        self.cb_adapter.send_command("hotkey_wssc.sh 0")
        self.cb_console.append("\n[KILL WSSC sent to CB]\n")

    def kill_ab_wssc(self):
        """Envía comando para matar WSSC en AB."""
        if not self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            return

        self.ab_adapter.send_command("hotkey_wssc.sh 0")
        self.ab_console.append("\n[KILL WSSC sent to AB]\n")

    # Asegúrate de tener este import arriba en tu archivo
    from core.services.diagnostic_engine import DiagnosticEngine
    from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox
    from PySide6.QtCore import QTimer
 
    # ============================================================
    # AB CONNECTION (Power On & Analyze)
    # ============================================================
 
    def start_ab_connection_analysis(self):
        """Ejecuta el flujo de AB CONNECTION y análisis en tiempo real."""
        
        if not self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            self.cb_console.append("\n[ERROR] CB NOT CONNECTED\n")
            return
 
        if not self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            self.ab_console.append("\n[ERROR] AB NOT CONNECTED\n")
            return
 
        self.cb_console.append("\n========== CB CONNECTION TEST ==========")
        self.ab_console.append("\n========== AB CONNECTION TEST ==========")
 
        self.cb_log_buffer.clear()
        self.ab_log_buffer.clear()
        self.cb_console.clear()
        self.ab_console.clear()
        
        self.cb_console.append("========== CB CONNECTION TEST ==========")
        self.ab_console.append("========== AB CONNECTION TEST ==========")
 
        self.cb_console.append("[CB] Enviando comando de encendido a la AB...")
        self.cb_adapter.send_command("pwr_on_intl.sh 1")
 
        self.cb_console.append("[DIAGNOSTIC] Monitoreando el arranque de la AB...")
        
        # Variables para el monitoreo en tiempo real
        self._diag_start_time = time.time()
        
        # Si ya había un timer corriendo, lo detenemos para que no se duplique
        if hasattr(self, '_diag_timer') and self._diag_timer.isActive():
            self._diag_timer.stop()
            
        self._diag_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._diag_timer.timeout.connect(self._check_ab_log_realtime)
        self._diag_timer.start(2000) # Revisar cada 2 segundos
    # ============================================================
    # CHECK AB LOG REALTIME
    # ============================================================
    def _check_ab_log_realtime(self):
        """Revisa el log. Si no hay falla clara, pregunta por los LEDs."""
        
        ab_log = self.ab_console.toPlainText()
        tiempo_transcurrido = time.time() - self._diag_start_time
 
        palabras_criticas = ["MRC", "ACPI MEMORY", "Kernel panic", "root@harman-si-intel-plk", "EXCEPTION"]
        ha_terminado_o_fallado = any(palabra in ab_log for palabra in palabras_criticas)
 
        # 2. Si terminó o pasaron más de 15 segundos (timeout)
        if ha_terminado_o_fallado or tiempo_transcurrido > 15:
            
            self._diag_timer.stop()
            
            # =========================================================
            #  SI NO HAY LOG O ES MUY CORTO, SALTAMOS AL FILTRO DE LEDS
            # =========================================================
            if not ab_log.strip() or len(ab_log) < 50:
                self.cb_console.append("\n[DIAGNOSTIC] ⚠️ No se recibió log de la AB en 15s.")
                self.cb_console.append("[DIAGNOSTIC] Solicitando estado de LEDs para diagnóstico...")
                
                # Lanzamos el diálogo de LEDs
                dialog = LedSelectorDialog(self)
                if dialog.exec():
                    led_d62, led_d65 = dialog.get_selection()
                    result = DiagnosticEngine.analyze_with_leds(ab_log, led_d62, led_d65)
                    self._show_diagnostic_result(result)
                else:
                    self.cb_console.append("\n[DIAGNOSTIC] Diagnóstico cancelado por el operador.")
                return
 
            # =========================================================
            #  FILTRO 1: Analizar SOLO el log (Si sí hubo log)
            # =========================================================
            result = DiagnosticEngine.analyze_ab_log_only(ab_log)
 
            # Si el log fue concluyente, no preguntamos por LEDs
            if result["status"] != "UNKNOWN":
                self._show_diagnostic_result(result)
                return
 
            # =========================================================
            #  FILTRO 2: Si el log no dijo nada, preguntamos por LEDs
            # =========================================================
            self.cb_console.append("[DIAGNOSTIC] Log no concluyente. Solicitando estado de LEDs...")
            dialog = LedSelectorDialog(self)
            if dialog.exec():
                led_d62, led_d65 = dialog.get_selection()
                result = DiagnosticEngine.analyze_with_leds(ab_log, led_d62, led_d65)
                self._show_diagnostic_result(result)
            else:
                self.cb_console.append("\n[DIAGNOSTIC] Diagnóstico cancelado por el operador.")
 
    def _show_diagnostic_result(self, result):
        """Muestra el resultado final en la consola y en una ventana emergente simplificada."""
        
        # 1. Escribir en la consola de la CB
        self.cb_console.append("\n" + "="*50)
        self.cb_console.append(f"🏥 DIAGNÓSTICO AUTOMÁTICO")
        self.cb_console.append(f"Modo de Falla: {result['mode']}")
        self.cb_console.append(f"Error: {result['diagnosis']}")
        self.cb_console.append("="*50 + "\n")
 
        # 2. Mostrar la ventana emergente final
        msg_final = QMessageBox()
        
        if result['status'] == "FAIL":
            msg_final.setIcon(QMessageBox.Critical)
            msg_final.setWindowTitle("Falla Detectada")
        elif result['status'] == "PASS":
            msg_final.setIcon(QMessageBox.Information)
            msg_final.setWindowTitle("Prueba Exitosa")
        else:
            msg_final.setIcon(QMessageBox.Warning)
            msg_final.setWindowTitle("Resultado Desconocido")
        
        # Mostramos solo el Modo y el Error (sin sugerencias)
        msg_final.setText(f"<b>Modo de Falla:</b> {result['mode']}")
        
        if result['status'] == "PASS":
                msg_final.setInformativeText("Arranque correcto.")
        elif result['mode'] == "UNKNOWN":
                msg_final.setInformativeText("No se pudo identificar el error. Requiere análisis manual.")
        else:
                msg_final.setInformativeText(f"<b>Error:</b>\n{result['diagnosis']}")
        
        msg_final.exec()
 
    # ============================================================
    # SAVE LOG
    # ============================================================

    def save_log(self):
        """Guarda el log combinado AB + CB en archivo."""
        # Crear carpeta si no existe
        os.makedirs("analysis_logs", exist_ok=True)

        # Timestamp
        from core.state.app_state import app_state

        sfc = getattr(
            app_state,
            "current_sfc",
            ""
        ).strip()

        project = (
            getattr(
                app_state,
                "project",
                "UNKNOWN"
            )
            .strip()
            .replace(" ", "_")
        )

        if sfc:

            filename = (
                f"analysis_logs/"
                f"{sfc}_{project}.log"
            )

        else:

            filename = (
                f"analysis_logs/"
                f"NO_SFC_{project}.log"
            )

        cb_content = "".join(self.cb_log_buffer)
        ab_content = "".join(self.ab_log_buffer)
        cb_content = cb_content.strip()
        ab_content = ab_content.strip()
        content = (
            "==================================================\n"
            "CB LOG\n"
            "==================================================\n\n"
            f"{cb_content}\n\n"
            "==================================================\n"
            "AB LOG\n"
            "==================================================\n\n"
            f"{ab_content}\n"
        )

        with open(filename, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(content)
        #
        # Limpiar buffers
        #
        self.cb_log_buffer.clear()
        self.ab_log_buffer.clear()

        #
        # Limpiar consolas
        #
        self.cb_console.clear()
        self.ab_console.clear()
        self.cb_console.append(f"\n[SAVE LOG] Saved to {filename}\n")
        self.ab_console.append(f"\n[SAVE LOG] Saved to {filename}\n")
  
    # ============================================================
    # DISCONNECT ALL (Solo cierra puertos y actualiza UI)
    # ============================================================
    def disconnect_all(self):
 
        # 1. Detener capturas
        self.cb_capture_running = False
        self.ab_capture_running = False
 
        # 2. Desconectar adaptadores
        if self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            self.ab_adapter.disconnect()
 
        if self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            self.cb_adapter.disconnect()
 
        # 3. Actualizar UI (Estado de los labels)
        self.lbl_cb_status.setText("STATUS: DISCONNECTED")
        self.lbl_cb_status.setStyleSheet("""
            color: #404040;
            font-weight: 600;
        """)
 
        self.lbl_ab_status.setText("STATUS: DISCONNECTED")
        self.lbl_ab_status.setStyleSheet("""
            color: #404040;
            font-weight: 600;
        """)
 
        # 4. Mensajes finales en consola
        self.cb_console.append("\n[CB DISCONNECTED]\n")
        self.ab_console.append("\n[AB DISCONNECTED]\n")
 
    # ============================================================
    # POWER OFF CB (Apagar placa y desconectar puertos)
    # ============================================================
    def power_off_cb(self):
        if not self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
            self.cb_console.append("\n[CB NOT CONNECTED]\n")
            return
 
        if not self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
            self.cb_console.append("\n[AB NOT CONNECTED, CANNOT ERASE DID]\n")
            return
 
        self.cb_console.append("\n[ERASE DID EN PROGRESO...]\n")
        self._power_off_response = ""
 
        def process_power_off():
            # 1. Mandar erase did a la AB y esperar el prompt de Linux
            erase_response = self.ab_adapter.execute_command_until(
                "websocket_test.sh erasedid",
                "root@harman-si-intel-plk:~#", # Esperamos el prompt
                60
            )
            self._power_off_response = erase_response
 
            # 2. Mandar el power off a la CB inmediatamente después
            self.cb_adapter.execute_command(
                "websocket_test.sh powerswitch 0"
            )
 
        worker = FixtureWorker(process_power_off)
 
        def on_finished(success):
            # Si la AB respondió, lo mostramos
            if self._power_off_response:
                self.ab_console.append(self._power_off_response)
 
            self.cb_console.append("\n[ERASE DID FINALIZADO]\n")
            self.cb_console.append("\n[POWER OFF COMMAND SENT TO CB]\n")
 
            # Desconectar adaptadores y actualizar UI
            # 1. Detener capturas
            self.cb_capture_running = False
            self.ab_capture_running = False
 
            # 2. Desconectar adaptadores
            if self.ab_adapter.is_connected():
                self.ab_adapter.disconnect()
 
            if self.cb_adapter.is_connected():
                self.cb_adapter.disconnect()
 
            # 3. Actualizar UI (Estado de los labels)
            self.lbl_cb_status.setText("STATUS: DISCONNECTED")
            self.lbl_cb_status.setStyleSheet("""
                color: #404040;
                font-weight: 600;
            """)
 
            self.lbl_ab_status.setText("STATUS: DISCONNECTED")
            self.lbl_ab_status.setStyleSheet("""
                color: #404040;
                font-weight: 600;
            """)
 
            # 4. Mensajes finales en consola
            self.cb_console.append("\n[CB DISCONNECTED]\n")
            self.ab_console.append("\n[AB DISCONNECTED]\n")
 
        def on_error(error_msg):
            self.cb_console.append(f"\n[ERROR EN EL HILO]: {error_msg}\n")
 
        worker.signals.finished.connect(on_finished)
        worker.signals.error.connect(on_error)
 
        from PySide6.QtCore import QThreadPool
        QThreadPool.globalInstance().start(worker)
 
 
    # ============================================================
    # DIAGNOSTIC ENGINE
    # ============================================================
    def run_diagnostics(self):
        """Analiza el log actual de la AB y muestra el diagnóstico."""
        
        # 1. Obtener el texto actual de la consola de la AB
        ab_log = self.ab_console.toPlainText()
        
        if not ab_log.strip():
            self.cb_console.append("\n[DIAGNOSTIC] ⚠️ No hay log en la AB para analizar.\n")
            return
 
        # 2. Ejecutar el motor de reglas
        result = DiagnosticEngine.analyze_ab_log(ab_log)
 
        # 3. Mostrar el resultado en la consola de la CB (o en un QMessageBox)
        self.cb_console.append("\n" + "="*50)
        self.cb_console.append(f"🏥 DIAGNÓSTICO AUTOMÁTICO (Modo {result['mode']})")
        self.cb_console.append(f"Estado: {result['status']}")
        self.cb_console.append(f"🔍 Problema: {result['diagnosis']}")
        self.cb_console.append(f"🛠️ Acción: {result['action']}")
        self.cb_console.append("="*50 + "\n")
 
        # Opcional: Mostrar una ventana emergente si es FAIL
        if result['status'] == "FAIL":
            from PySide6.QtWidgets import QMessageBox
            msg = QMessageBox()
            msg.setIcon(QMessageBox.Critical)
            msg.setWindowTitle(f"Falla Detectada - Modo {result['mode']}")
            msg.setText(f"Problema: {result['diagnosis']}")
            msg.setInformativeText(f"Acción Sugerida:\n{result['action']}")
            msg.exec()
 
    # ============================================================
    # CLEAR TERMINAL
    # ============================================================
    def clear_terminal(self):

        self.cb_console.clear()

        self.ab_console.clear()

        self.cb_log_buffer.clear()

        self.ab_log_buffer.clear()

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QLabel,
    QPushButton,
    QCheckBox,
    QRadioButton,
    QTextBrowser
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from core.ui.styles import AppStyles
 

class ResultsView(QWidget):

    def __init__(self):

        super().__init__()

        self.last_html = ""
        self.last_json = ""

        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        #
        # Export Section
        #
        export_label = QLabel(
            "Export formats"
        )

        layout.addWidget(
            export_label
        )

        export_layout = QHBoxLayout()

        self.chk_export_html = QCheckBox(
            "HTML"
        )

        self.chk_export_json = QCheckBox(
            "JSON"
        )

        self.chk_export_html.setChecked(
            True
        )

        self.chk_export_json.setChecked(
            True
        )

        export_layout.addWidget(
            self.chk_export_html
        )

        export_layout.addWidget(
            self.chk_export_json
        )

        export_layout.addStretch()

        layout.addLayout(
            export_layout
        )

        #
        # Preview Section
        #
        preview_label = QLabel(
            "Preview"
        )

        layout.addWidget(
            preview_label
        )

        preview_layout = QHBoxLayout()

        self.rb_show_html = QRadioButton(
            "HTML"
        )

        self.rb_show_json = QRadioButton(
            "JSON"
        )

        self.rb_show_html.setChecked(
            True
        )

        self.rb_show_html.toggled.connect(
            self.update_preview
        )

        self.rb_show_json.toggled.connect(
            self.update_preview
        )

        preview_layout.addWidget(
            self.rb_show_html
        )

        preview_layout.addWidget(
            self.rb_show_json
        )

        preview_layout.addStretch()

        layout.addLayout(
            preview_layout
        )

        # =====================================
        # EXPORT BUTTON
        # =====================================
        
        self.btn_export = QPushButton("EXPORT RESULTS")
        self.btn_export.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.EXPORT_RESULTS_BUTTON)
        self.btn_export.setFixedWidth(180)
        self.btn_export.setFixedHeight(36) # Altura fija para mantener simetría
        self.btn_export.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor) # Manito al pasar el mouse
 
        # Layout para centrar el botón
        button_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        button_layout.addStretch()
        button_layout.addWidget(self.btn_export)
        button_layout.addStretch()
 
        # Agregar al layout principal (no lo agregues suelto y luego en el HBox)
        layout.addLayout(button_layout)
 
        #
        # Preview Browser
        #
        self.browser = QTextBrowser()

        self.browser.setOpenExternalLinks(
            True
        )

        self.browser.setStyleSheet("""
        QTextBrowser {
            background: white;
            border: 1px solid #D0D0D0;
        }
        """)

        layout.addWidget(
            self.browser
        )

    def set_export_callback(
        self,
        callback
    ):

        self.btn_export.clicked.connect(
            callback
        )

    def set_html(
        self,
        html
    ):

        self.last_html = html

        self.update_preview()

    def set_json(
        self,
        json_text
    ):

        self.last_json = json_text

        self.update_preview()

    def update_preview(self):

        if self.rb_show_html.isChecked():

            self.browser.setHtml(
                self.last_html
            )

        else:

            self.browser.setPlainText(
                self.last_json
            )

    def export_html_enabled(
        self
    ):

        return (
            self.chk_export_html
            .isChecked()
        )

    def export_json_enabled(
        self
    ):

        return (
            self.chk_export_json
            .isChecked()
        )

#!/usr/bin/env python3

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QGroupBox,
    QListWidget,
    QPushButton,
    QTextEdit,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QWidget,
)
from core.ui.styles import AppStyles
from core.ui.widgets.fixture_control_widget import (
    FixtureControlWidget
)

class RunnerView:

    @staticmethod
    def create(
        fixture_controller,
        button_repo,
        test_controller,
        log_callback,
        show_details_callback,
        run_selected_callback,
        run_all_callback
    ):
        tab = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(tab)

        #
        # Fixture Control
        #
        fixture_widget = FixtureControlWidget(
            fixture_controller
        )

        layout.addWidget(
            fixture_widget
        )

        #
        # Test List
        #
        tests_group = QGroupBox(
            "Tests"
        )

        tests_layout = QVBoxLayout(
            tests_group
        )

        test_list = QListWidget()

        for button in button_repo.get_all():

            test_list.addItem(
                button["label"]
            )

            test_controller.test_status[
                button["label"]
            ] = "NOT_RUN"

        test_list.itemClicked.connect(
            show_details_callback
        )

        tests_layout.addWidget(
            test_list
        )

        layout.addWidget(
            tests_group
        )

        #
        # Actions
        #

        btn_execute = QPushButton(
            "▶ RUN SELECTED TEST"
        )

        btn_execute.clicked.connect(
            run_selected_callback
        )

        btn_execute.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_TEST_BUTTON
        )

        layout.addWidget(
            btn_execute
        )

        btn_all = QPushButton(
            "▶ RUN ALL SEQUENCE"
        )

        btn_all.clicked.connect(
            run_all_callback
        )

        btn_all.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.RUN_ALL_BUTTON
        )

        layout.addWidget(
            btn_all
        )
        #
        # Results
        #
        results_group = QGroupBox(
            "Log / Results"
        )

        results_layout = QVBoxLayout(
            results_group
        )

        result_box = QTextEdit()

        result_box.setReadOnly(
            True
        )

        result_box.setFontFamily(
            "Courier New"
        )

        result_box.setFontPointSize(
            10
        )

        results_layout.addWidget(
            result_box
        )

        layout.addWidget(
            results_group
        )

        return (
            tab,
            fixture_widget,
            test_list,
            result_box
        )

ARCHIVO: core\ui\widgets\collapsible_group.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QPushButton
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt

class CollapsibleGroup(QWidget):

    def __init__(self, title: str):
        super().__init__()

        self._title = title
        self._expanded = True
        self._summary = ""
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        self.btn_toggle = QPushButton(
            f"▼ {title}"
        )
        self.btn_toggle.setFixedWidth(
            350
        )
        self.btn_toggle.setCursor(
            Qt.PointingHandCursor
        )

        self.btn_toggle.clicked.connect(
            self.toggle
        )

        from core.ui.styles import AppStyles

        self.btn_toggle.setStyleSheet(
            AppStyles.GROUP_HEADER
        )
        
        self.main_layout.addWidget(
            self.btn_toggle,
            alignment=Qt.AlignCenter
        )

        self.content = QWidget()

        self.content_layout = QVBoxLayout(
            self.content
        )

        # Iniciar cerrado
        self._expanded = False

        self.content.hide()

        self.btn_toggle.setText(
            f"▶ {self._title}"
        )

        self.main_layout.addWidget(
            self.content
        )

    def toggle(self):

        self._expanded = not self._expanded

        self.content.setVisible(
            self._expanded
        )

        self.refresh_title()

    def update_summary(
        self,
        passed: int,
        total: int
    ):

        self._summary = (
            f"({passed}/{total} PASS)"
        )

        self.refresh_title()

    def refresh_title(self):

        icon = (
            "▼"
            if self._expanded
            else "▶"
        )

        self.btn_toggle.setText(
            f"{icon} {self._title} {self._summary}"
        )

ARCHIVO: core\ui\widgets\fixture_control_widget.py

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget,
    QGroupBox,
    QPushButton,
    QVBoxLayout,
    QLineEdit,
    QHBoxLayout,
    QMessageBox
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QThreadPool, Signal
from core.ui.widgets.progress_bar_indicator import ProgressBarIndicator
from core.ui.widgets.status_indicator import StatusIndicator
from core.ui.styles import AppStyles
from core.ui.workers.fixture_worker import FixtureWorker
 
 
class FixtureControlWidget(QWidget):
    
    # 🔥 Señal para comunicar el hilo secundario con el hilo principal de la UI
    _status_signal = Signal(str)
 
    def __init__(
        self,
        fixture_controller,
        run_all_button=None,
        search_callback=None
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.thread_pool = QThreadPool.globalInstance()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
 
        self.fixture_controller = fixture_controller
        self.run_all_button = run_all_button
        self.search_callback = search_callback
        self._active_worker = None
 
        # Conectar la señal al método que actualiza la UI (se ejecutará en el hilo principal)
        self._status_signal.connect(self._update_ui_status)
 
        # 1. INDICADOR OCULTO (Requerido por el FixtureController)
        self.fixture_status = StatusIndicator("NOT CONNECTED")
        self.fixture_status.setVisible(False)
        
        self.fixture_controller.register_indicator(self.fixture_status)
        self.fixture_controller.register_status_widget(self)
 
        group = QGroupBox("Fixture Control")
        group_layout = QHBoxLayout(group)
        group_layout.setContentsMargins(10, 20, 10, 10) 
        group_layout.setSpacing(10)
 
        # 2. BOTÓN CONNECT
        self.btn_connect = QPushButton("CONNECT")
        self.btn_connect.setFixedSize(140, 32)
        self.btn_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.FIXTURE_BUTTON_NOT_CONNECTED)
        self.btn_connect.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_connect.clicked.connect(self.connect_fixture)
        self.fixture_controller.register_connect_button(self.btn_connect)
 
        # 3. SPINNER CIRCULAR
        self.spinner = ProgressBarIndicator()
 
        # 4. BOTÓN RUN ALL
        if self.run_all_button:
            self.run_all_button.setFixedSize(120, 32)
 
        # 5. BUSCADOR
        self.search_box = QLineEdit()
        self.search_box.setPlaceholderText("Search test...")
        self.search_box.setFixedSize(200, 32)
        self.search_box.setStyleSheet("""
            QLineEdit {
                background: white;
                border: 1px solid #D0D0D0;
                border-radius: 6px;
                padding: 4px 8px;
            }
        """)
        if self.search_callback:
            self.search_box.textChanged.connect(self.search_callback)
 
        # ===== AGREGAR A LA LÍNEA =====
        group_layout.addWidget(self.btn_connect)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.spinner)
        group_layout.addStretch()
        group_layout.addWidget(self.run_all_button if self.run_all_button else QWidget())
        group_layout.addWidget(self.search_box)
 
        self.btn_connect.setEnabled(False)
        layout.addWidget(group)
        
        # Inicializamos la UI
        self.set_fixture_status("NOT CONNECTED")
 
 
    def connect_fixture(self):
        # Evitar doble clic
        if self._active_worker is not None:
            return 
 
        # Determinar si conectamos o desconectamos según el texto del botón
        if self.btn_connect.text() in ["DISCONNECT", "CONNECTED"]:
            worker = FixtureWorker(self.fixture_controller.disconnect_fixture)
        else:
            worker = FixtureWorker(self.fixture_controller.auto_connect_fixture)
        
        # Conectar las señales del worker
        worker.signals.error.connect(self._on_worker_error)
        worker.signals.finished.connect(self._on_worker_finished)
        self._active_worker = worker
        
        self.thread_pool.start(worker)
 
 
    def _on_worker_error(self, error_msg):
        print(f"Error en fixture: {error_msg}")
        # El estado se actualizará a FAILED a través del controlador, pero por si acaso forzamos
        self._status_signal.emit("FAILED")
        self._active_worker = None
 
 
    def _on_worker_finished(self, success):
        self._active_worker = None
 
 
    def set_fixture_status(self, status: str):
        # Este método es llamado por el controlador desde OTRO HILO.
        # Emitimos la señal para que se procese de forma segura en el hilo principal.
        self._status_signal.emit(status)
 
 
    def _update_ui_status(self, status: str):
        self.fixture_status.set_status(status)
 
        if status == "CONNECTING":
            self.btn_connect.setEnabled(False)
            self.btn_connect.setText("CONNECTING...")
            self.btn_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.FIXTURE_BUTTON_CONNECTING)
            self.spinner.start("#F59E0B")
            return
            
        if status == "DISCONNECTING":
            self.btn_connect.setEnabled(False)
            self.btn_connect.setText("DISCONNECTING...")
            self.btn_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.FIXTURE_BUTTON_CONNECTING)
            self.spinner.start("#DC2626")
            return
 
        self.spinner.stop()
        self.btn_connect.setEnabled(True)
 
        if status == "NOT CONNECTED":
            self.btn_connect.setText("CONNECT")
            self.btn_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.FIXTURE_BUTTON_NOT_CONNECTED)
 
        elif status == "READY":
            self.btn_connect.setText("DISCONNECT") 
            self.btn_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.FIXTURE_BUTTON_READY)
 
        elif status == "FAILED":
            self.btn_connect.setText("CONNECTION FAILED") 
            self.btn_connect.setStyleSheet(AppStyles.FIXTURE_BUTTON_FAILED)

import os
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
    QCheckBox, QPushButton, QFrame
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from PySide6.QtGui import QPixmap
 
class LedSelectorDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Diagnóstico AB")
        self.setFixedSize(480, 250) # Ventana más ancha para acomodar la imagen
        self.setStyleSheet("background-color: #F3F2F1; font-family: 'Segoe UI';")
        
        # Resultados
        self.led_d62 = None
        self.led_d65 = None
 
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
 
        # Título y mensaje
        title = QLabel("Se detectó un fallo en el arranque de la AB.")
        title.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; font-size: 12px; color: #323130;")
        main_layout.addWidget(title)
        
        info = QLabel("Selecciona el estado final de los LEDs según la referencia:")
        info.setStyleSheet("color: #605E5C; margin-bottom: 5px;")
        main_layout.addWidget(info)
 
        # =========================================================
        #  CONTENEDOR HORIZONTAL (Imagen a la izquierda, Tabla a la derecha)
        # =========================================================
        content_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        content_layout.setSpacing(15)
 
        # 1. Imagen de referencia (Izquierda)
        self.image_label = QLabel()
        # Busca la imagen en la carpeta assets de tu proyecto
        image_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", "..", "assets", "leds_reference.png")
        
        if os.path.exists(image_path):
            pixmap = QPixmap(image_path)
            # Escalamos la imagen para que no haga la ventana gigante
            self.image_label.setPixmap(pixmap.scaled(200, 150, Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.SmoothTransformation))
        else:
            self.image_label.setText("[Imagen no encontrada]\nVerifica assets/leds_reference.png")
            self.image_label.setStyleSheet("border: 1px dashed #ccc; padding: 10px; color: #999;")
        
        content_layout.addWidget(self.image_label)
 
        # 2. Mini Tabla de LEDs (Derecha)
        table_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        table_layout.setSpacing(10)
 
        # Columna D62
        col_d62 = QVBoxLayout()
        lbl_d62 = QLabel("D62")
        lbl_d62.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: #323130;")
        lbl_d62.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.cb_d62_high = QCheckBox("HIGH")
        self.cb_d62_low = QCheckBox("LOW")
        col_d62.addWidget(lbl_d62)
        col_d62.addWidget(self.cb_d62_high)
        col_d62.addWidget(self.cb_d62_low)
        col_d62.addStretch() # Empuja los checkboxes hacia arriba
        table_layout.addLayout(col_d62)
 
        # Línea separadora vertical
        line = QFrame()
        line.setFrameShape(QFrame.VLine)
        line.setStyleSheet("color: #D2D0CE;")
        table_layout.addWidget(line)
 
        # Columna D65
        col_d65 = QVBoxLayout()
        lbl_d65 = QLabel("D65")
        lbl_d65.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; color: #323130;")
        lbl_d65.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.cb_d65_high = QCheckBox("HIGH")
        self.cb_d65_low = QCheckBox("LOW")
        col_d65.addWidget(lbl_d65)
        col_d65.addWidget(self.cb_d65_high)
        col_d65.addWidget(self.cb_d65_low)
        col_d65.addStretch()
        table_layout.addLayout(col_d65)
 
        content_layout.addLayout(table_layout)
        main_layout.addLayout(content_layout)
        
        main_layout.addSpacing(10)
 
        # Botón de Confirmar
        btn_confirm = QPushButton("Continuar")
        btn_confirm.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #2D3748; color: white; font-weight: bold; 
                padding: 8px; border-radius: 4px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #4A5568; }
        """)
        btn_confirm.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        main_layout.addWidget(btn_confirm)
 
        # Lógica para que solo se pueda seleccionar un HIGH o LOW por LED
        self.cb_d62_high.toggled.connect(self._on_d62_toggled)
        self.cb_d62_low.toggled.connect(self._on_d62_toggled)
        self.cb_d65_high.toggled.connect(self._on_d65_toggled)
        self.cb_d65_low.toggled.connect(self._on_d65_toggled)
 
    def _on_d62_toggled(self):
        if self.cb_d62_high.isChecked():
            self.cb_d62_low.setChecked(False)
        elif self.cb_d62_low.isChecked():
            self.cb_d62_high.setChecked(False)
 
    def _on_d65_toggled(self):
        if self.cb_d65_high.isChecked():
            self.cb_d65_low.setChecked(False)
        elif self.cb_d65_low.isChecked():
            self.cb_d65_high.setChecked(False)
 
    def get_selection(self):
        """Retorna el estado de los LEDs seleccionados."""
        d62 = "HIGH" if self.cb_d62_high.isChecked() else "LOW" if self.cb_d62_low.isChecked() else "UNKNOWN"
        d65 = "HIGH" if self.cb_d65_high.isChecked() else "LOW" if self.cb_d65_low.isChecked() else "UNKNOWN"
        return d62, d65

ARCHIVO: core\ui\widgets\progress_bar_indicator.py
================================================================================

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget
from PySide6.QtCore import QTimer, Qt, QRectF
from PySide6.QtGui import QPainter, QColor, QPen
 
 
class ProgressBarIndicator(QWidget):
    """Spinner circular de carga (estilo iOS/Windows)"""
 
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._angle = 0
        self._is_running = False
        self._color = QColor("#F59E0B") # Naranja por defecto
        
        self._timer = QTimer(self)
        self._timer.timeout.connect(self._animate)
        self._timer.setInterval(30) # Velocidad de giro
        
        # Tamaño fijo cuadrado para que sea un círculo perfecto
        self.setFixedSize(24, 24)
        self.setVisible(False)
 
    def start(self, color_hex: str = "#F59E0B"):
        self._color = QColor(color_hex)
        self._is_running = True
        self.setVisible(True)
        self._timer.start()
 
    def stop(self):
        self._is_running = False
        self._timer.stop()
        self.setVisible(False)
 
    def _animate(self):
        # Girar 10 grados cada 30ms
        self._angle = (self._angle - 10) % 360
        self.update()
 
    def paintEvent(self, event):
        if not self._is_running:
            return
 
        painter = QPainter(self)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
 
        rect = QRectF(2, 2, self.width() - 4, self.height() - 4)
 
        # 1. Fondo del círculo (gris claro)
        bg_pen = QPen(QColor("#E5E7EB"))
        bg_pen.setWidth(3)
        bg_pen.setCapStyle(Qt.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(bg_pen)
        painter.drawEllipse(rect)
 
        # 2. Arco de color girando
        color_pen = QPen(self._color)
        color_pen.setWidth(3)
        color_pen.setCapStyle(Qt.RoundCap)
        painter.setPen(color_pen)
        
        # Dibujar un arco de 90 grados (el 25% del círculo)
        # Qt usa grados * 16 para dibujar arcos
        painter.drawArc(rect, self._angle * 16, 90 * 16)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Indicador de estado con colores"""

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt

class StatusIndicator(QLabel):
    """Indicador visual de estado"""
    
    COLORS = {
        "NOT CONNECTED": "#F1F0EC",
        "CONNECTING": "#FFC107",
        "READY": "#4CAF50",
        "FAILED": "#F44336",
        "RUNNING": "#FF9800"
    }
    
    def __init__(self, initial_status: str = "NOT CONNECTED", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._status = initial_status
        self.set_status(initial_status)
    
    def set_status(self, status: str):

        self._status = status

        styles = {

            "NOT CONNECTED": "⚫ NOT CONNECTED",
            "CONNECTING": "🟡 CONNECTING",
            "READY": "🟢 READY",
            "FAILED": "🔴 FAILED",
            "RUNNING": "🟠 RUNNING",
        }

        self.setText(
            styles.get(status, status)
        )

        self.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)

        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel {
                background-color: #F8F8F8;
                color: black;
                border-radius: 6px;
                border: 1px solid #C0C0C0;
                padding: 6px 12px;
                font-weight: bold;
            }
        """)
    @property
    def current_status(self):
        return self._status

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Widget de tarjeta de prueba para dashboard"""

from PySide6.QtCore import Qt,Signal
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QPushButton


class TestCard(QPushButton):
    """Tarjeta de prueba en el dashboard"""
    rightClicked = Signal(str) 
    # Colores por estado
    COLORS = {
        "NOT_RUN": "#F5F5F5",
        "RUNNING": "#FFE699",
        "PASS": "#C6EFCE",
        "FAIL": "#FFC7CE",
        "WARNING": "#FFE699"
    }
    
    def __init__(self, test_name: str, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.test_name = test_name
        self._status = "NOT_RUN"
        self.setFixedSize(170, 80)
        self.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.update_display()
    
    def set_status(self, status: str):
        """Actualiza el estado de la tarjeta"""
        self._status = status
        self.update_display()
    
    def update_display(self):
        """Actualiza la apariencia de la tarjeta"""

        color = self.COLORS.get(
            self._status,
            "#F1F0EC"
        )

        self.setText(
            f"{self.test_name}\n\n{self._status}"
        )

        self.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QPushButton {{

                background-color: {color};

                color: #202020;

                border: 1px solid #2D3748;
                border-radius: 0px;

                font-family: "Segoe UI";
                font-size: 9pt;
                font-weight: 500;

                padding: 6px;
                text-align: center;
            }}

            QPushButton:hover {{

                border: 2px solid #3182CE;
            }}

            QPushButton:pressed {{

                background-color: #DDEAF2;
            }}
        """)

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):

        if event.button() == Qt.RightButton:

            self.rightClicked.emit(
                self.test_name
            )

            return

        super().mousePressEvent(event)

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: core\ui\workers\fixture_worker.py
================================================================================

from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QRunnable, Signal
import traceback
 
class FixtureSignals(QObject):
    finished = Signal(bool)
    error = Signal(str)
    status = Signal(str)
 
class FixtureWorker(QRunnable):
    def __init__(self, task):
        super().__init__()
        self.task = task
        self.signals = FixtureSignals()
        self.setAutoDelete(False) 
 
    def run(self):
        try:
            self.task()
            self.signals.finished.emit(True)
        except Exception as e:
            # Imprime el error en la consola para que lo veas
            print(f"ERROR EN WORKER: {str(e)}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
            self.signals.error.emit(str(e))

ARCHIVO: core\ui\workers\test_worker.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Worker para ejecutar pruebas en hilos separados"""

import traceback
from collections.abc import Callable

from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, QRunnable, QThreadPool, Signal


class WorkerSignals(QObject):
    """Señales para comunicación entre hilos"""

    log = Signal(str)
    progress = Signal(int, int)
    finished = Signal(object)
    error = Signal(str)


class TestRunnable(QRunnable):
    """Runnable para ejecutar pruebas en hilo separado"""
    
    def __init__(self, func: Callable, *args, **kwargs):
        super().__init__()
        self.func = func
        self.args = args
        self.kwargs = kwargs
        self.signals = WorkerSignals()
        self._is_running = True
        self.setAutoDelete(True)
    
    def run(self):
        """Ejecuta la función en el hilo"""
        try:
            self.signals.log.emit("🚀 Hilo iniciado")
            result = self.func(
                *self.args,
                **self.kwargs
            )
            

            self.signals.finished.emit(result)
            self.signals.log.emit("✅ Hilo finalizado")
        except Exception as e:
            error_msg = f"❌ Error: {e!s}\n{traceback.format_exc()}"
            try:
                self.signals.error.emit(error_msg)
                self.signals.log.emit(error_msg)
            except RuntimeError:
                # Las señales ya fueron destruidas, ignorar
                pass
        finally:
            # Limpiar señales
            try:
                self.signals.deleteLater()
            except:
                pass
    
    def stop(self):
        """Detiene la ejecución (flag)"""
        self._is_running = False


def run_test_in_thread(func: Callable, *args, on_finished: Callable | None = None,
                       on_log: Callable | None = None, on_error: Callable | None = None,
                       on_progress: Callable | None = None) -> TestRunnable:
    """Ejecuta una función en un hilo separado"""

    runnable = TestRunnable(func, *args)
    
    # Conectar señales
    if on_finished:
        runnable.signals.finished.connect(on_finished)
    if on_log:
        runnable.signals.log.connect(on_log)
    if on_error:
        runnable.signals.error.connect(on_error)
    if on_progress:
        runnable.signals.progress.connect(on_progress)

        
    # Ejecutar en thread pool
    QThreadPool.globalInstance().start(runnable)
    
    return runnable

ARCHIVO: core\utils\resource_path.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3

import sys
from pathlib import Path


def resource_path(relative_path: str) -> Path:
    """
    Compatible con Python normal y PyInstaller
    """

    if hasattr(
        sys,
        "_MEIPASS"
    ):
        base_path = Path(
            sys._MEIPASS
        )
    else:
        base_path = Path(
            __file__
        ).resolve().parents[2]

    return base_path / relative_path
